## What exists right now (flow you can walk)

`/` Registration (license code + on-screen keyboard) → `/LoadingResources`
(real boot incl. the Xeno loyalty partner; 401 ⇒ auto-logout) → `/start` Figma
splash (+ hidden 3s top-left hold ⇒ Activity Center) → tap ⇒ `/second` WHERE
ARE YOU EATING (CX pipelines) → with loyalty on ⇒ `/phone` (numpad lookup,
skippable) ⇒ rewards auto-open
→ pick ⇒ real menu fetch+convert ⇒ `/menu` (rail, cards, UNAVAILABLE for a sold-out item or one no guest can complete, totals bar — its TOTAL is the bag's own Total; the rail lists the sub-categories and the pane shows one section at a time when posistKiosk's menu settings say so) →
item tap ⇒ `/customization` PDP (single product, variant fast-lane, pack slot
cards + SELECT sheets + tier-2 nested customize; every option card says CHOOSE /
REMOVE, and one whose item has options of its own adds CUSTOMIZE once picked —
the posistKiosk flow: a required one's CHOOSE opens its customize page;
sold-out options, sizes and slots read Unavailable; every group the PDP can refuse
counts "N of M" — "0 of 0" takes no pick) ⇒ ADD TO BAG; upsell-tagged
items ⇒ MIAM prompt (accept ⇒ pack PDP · decline ⇒ item by shape); re-tap of
an in-cart customizable ⇒ repeat sheet; every menu add (a plain one too, and
the +1 on a row already in the bag — since 2026-10-14) ⇒ YOUR PRODUCT HAS BEEN
ADDED (nothing to suggest: the title, the Total and GOT IT); with suggestions it
STAGES (posistKiosk): a tap anywhere on a
suggestion card (or its +) picks it — an "N selected" pill between a "−" and
the "+", the Total previews the order; a sized / customizable / pack suggestion
hides the modal (picks kept) and opens Select a Size / the PDP (never the
repeat sheet, and no rail of its own), whose CONTINUE / ADD TO BAG parks the
item back in it; nothing reaches the bag until ADD TO ORDER buys the picks
(nothing to buy: the same button reads NO THANKS / GOT IT and only closes); X
and the scrim throw them away → `/cart` MY BAG sheet (rows, edit,
remove-confirm, Complete-Your-Meal rail, Rewards section → REWARDS sheet
(radio + SAVE SELECTION, locked-gap nudges, suggested-items rail, freebie
picker), Discounts line — a least-value offer's rows wear its Free / N% off
chip —, Sub Total/Total via bill engine) ⇒ PAY preflight (busy while it checks;
a sold-out item, size or pick is named — "Out of stock: …" — and stays flagged
in its row until Edit or remove) ⇒ `/tent` (table tabs) / `/phone` / `/customerName` ⇒ `/payment`
(every BACK returns to the step before it that this session used — `useCheckoutFlow`)
⇒ PAY AT COUNTER ⇒ "Do you want to print the receipt?" YES / NO ⇒ order pushed ⇒ `/orderSuccess` (#order
number; START NEW ORDER counts 20 s down from arrival — a touch never extends it; a tap or 0 ⇒ /start, whose mount runs the full reset).
VIEW MY BAG ⇒ `/cart`. MY REWARDS (n) on the menu ⇒ rewards sheet ⇒ redeem
(validate → OTP → claim) ⇒ free/discounted reward row in the bag. Cancel Order ⇒ confirm ⇒ Xeno revoke
⇒ full session reset ⇒ `/start`. Cart survives a crash/reload via Dexie
rehydrate.
**Paytm (P8b):** `/payment` adds PAY WITH CARD BELOW (EDC) and PAY WITH UPI QR
(DQR) when configured; a tile arms ⇒ the 3 s window (CANCEL) ⇒ the initiate on
`/payment` (ids stored and flushed first; PleaseWait at once; the failure modal
over the tiles — CLOSE · TRY AGAIN, or CLOSE alone for a local refusal; CLOSE
closes it in place) ⇒ `/paymentPolling` settles
by status (EDC instructions or the QR, countdown, CANCEL, not-paid / unknown
staff panels) ⇒ paid ⇒ the session's ids cleared (flushed) ⇒ "Do you want to
print the receipt?" AFTER the payment (no BACK; idle held; NO by itself at
T − 20 s; skipped as NO after a reload, which drops the local order record) ⇒
Order Complete. PAY AT COUNTER still asks before the push. `/payment` swallows
taps for 500 ms on arrival and after CANCEL / CLOSE (a double tap
never arms the tile under it). A reload resumes the SAME ids; `/start` releases
an open session; idle is held (≤240 s).
**Splash (P9d):** the boot stores the operator's getMedia `home_screen` list
(this deployment's entries only); the splash shows WELCOME (Figma 1:5617)
with no media, the 1:2203 centre card alone for one slide (user decision
2026-10-13; was the full-bleed 1:5604, which put START ORDER over the
operator video's own captions), and the 1:2203
peek carousel for two or more (images dwell `iteration_time` s, ≥3 s, else
8 s; videos play `iteration_time` times). A slide that errors, stalls 15 s or
has no decodable video is skipped for the visit, so the splash degrades
carousel → single card → WELCOME and is never blank. A media failure at boot
keeps the last-known media and never blocks the boot.
**Updates (P9e):** a new build (service worker), an FCM brand push, or boot
data older than 6 h only ever applies on the splash: after 15 s untouched a
5 s countdown shows, then the kiosk reloads (new build) or re-runs the boot in
REFRESH mode (push / schedule / the Activity Center's "Reload resources");
a guest tap defers it, the Activity Center pauses it, offline skips it. The
boot commits atomically, so a failed refresh returns to the splash on the old
data and retries after 30 min. The Activity Center shows when the data last
loaded. The FCM token, version report and update ack can no longer log out or
de-register the kiosk. FCM stays inert until Firebase keys exist AND
notification permission is pre-granted (it never prompts).
Language switch EN↔العربية anywhere via the footer (RTL text, LTR layout — P9f; the footer's flag follows the language: GB for English, none for Arabic).
**Idle (P9a):** on every screen from `/second` on, 100 s without a touch opens
"You have been inactive" (START AGAIN fills over 20 s · CONTINUE ORDERING or a
backdrop tap re-arms); at 120 s (or `ideal_time` if shorter, floor 60 s) the
kiosk returns to `/start`. `/start`'s mount ends EVERY session (Xeno revoke →
full reset), so idle, footer cancels, NotFound and reloads all land clean, and
the next customer starts in English. Idle is held while an order push or a
loyalty call is in flight (capped at 120 s) and on `/orderSuccess` (its own
20 s timer owns that exit).
**ADA (P9c):** when the tenant enables `accessibility_mode`, FOOTER "ADA
DISPLAY" (aria-pressed; a white pill when on) lowers the whole session into the bottom 1122 px — a
brand zone (texture + logo lockup) fills the top 798 px and taps there exit —
with nothing scaled, every control inside reach, sheets/modals capped to the
zone, the bag at 765 px, and the loyalty rewards auto-dismiss off (WCAG 2.2.1).
Toggling never remounts a screen; the splash, boot and registration are always
full-stage, and every return to the splash ends ADA for the next guest.

What kept them optional — lane walk-cards-prices' engine-less calls (9066718) — merged as aa1e67b, so: make both required in `offerRevalidation.ts`, drop the engine-less test cases, delete the block (an SDK edit, with that file's commit — the SDK-commit row)

| **`enable_dis_addon` and the offers' "Save" figure (lane walk-offers, review fix, 2026-10-14):** TB DOES pass `discountOnAddon` to `redeemGetItem` (`useCartHook.redeemGetItem` → `discountOnAddonRdx`, set from the deployment setting `enable_dis_addon`), and the order then discounts a get item's add-ons too — so a free £9 burger with +£2 bacon saves £11 under an "exact £9" quote (the lane builder's "TB never passes discountOnAddon" was wrong). Built: `rankOffersForKiosk(offers, ctx, isOnMenu, addonsDiscounted = false)`, filled by useOfferSavings from `selectDiscountOnAddon`; with the setting on, an offer whose reward the guest builds with add-ons (a customizable entity, or an open size whose sizes carry modifier groups — never a fixed size or a plain item) quotes NO "Save" figure (its row shows the mechanic or description line) and ranks last among the "up to" rows; off, nothing changes. posistKiosk never priced customizable freebies either. Alternative: a modelled ceiling (the modifier map, the punch rules and tier-2 resolution in the ranker), which would read high ("up to" every add-on free). Also: is `enable_dis_addon` on for this tenant? If so, rows like "And Offer With Varaint" (its Chinese group holds the customizable 5 Meat Medium) show their description instead of "Save up to ﷼6.20" | user / client | ⚠️ open |

| **Out of stock and group limits — open calls (lanes walk-oos, group-limits, dead-offer, inactive-groups; 2026-10-14/15):** (1) dead-offer: a mixed "or" buy side (one entry alive, one dead) keeps its locked row; in its stage the dead entry is a greyed Unavailable tile with no add target and only the live ones add — while an entry already stamped sold out when the offers loaded is left out of the stage. Leave a dead entry out too, so the stage lists only what can be added?; (2) dead-offer (7ef8270, for sign-off): least-value and group-wise buy sides are unions, the converter's sold-out stamp included — so a stamped "and" entry beside a live one is now a locked row (it was struck) and one beside an entry off the menu is listed (it was hidden); posistKiosk strikes or hides both (its `buySideIsDead` reads the relation for every item offer); it matters only if real least-value / group-wise payloads carry relation "and" (no capture shows it; the fixtures carry none); (3) oos-2: a sized item whose sizes are only SOLD OUT (none dead) keeps a live card — "+" opens Select a Size with every size greyed (focus on the X) and its PDP never opens a size; show the Unavailable card, as a dead one now does (611fe86)?; (4) oos-2 (money path — go-ahead needed): an offer whose freebies are ALL dead opens an empty freebie picker (CONFIRM off, X closes, nothing written, no money moves); the fix strips dead freebies in `useOfferApply.selectOfferAndCommit`, so SAVE answers "noGetItems" like an all-sold-out offer; (5) inactive-groups F2: on the Edit path the customizable commit and tier-2 SAVE (`findFirstMinMaxViolation`, `findTier2MinMaxViolation`) still count a group switched off after the add — they read every key the row holds, never isActive — so UPDATE is refused under a group the guest cannot see and only Remove gets out; one guard per scan closes it (`if (!properties[0]?.isActive) continue;` in groupCompletion.ts and tier2Logic.ts — the fork's customizable commit and tier-2 SAVE would then accept such a row too), or accept it as a known edge; (6) inactive-groups F3: an OPTION with no isActive key — the PDP lists it, both commits sum it and the dead rule offers it (`isActive !== false`), but PAY counts a pick only when `isActive` is truthy (legacy), so its row is refused at every PAY and Edit shows it still picked; one rule: (a) PAY counts a pick unless `isActive === false` (the fork sees it too), or (b) the PDP, tier-2 and the dead rule hide a keyless option (the fork's `getAvailableConstituentItems`); only an API that omits the key triggers it (slim-menu sets it on all 111 options); (7) group-limits (Rule 1): the no-max add guard lives in TB (`useCustomization` `hasNoMax` / `refusesAdd`, and `openTier2New`) — move it into the SDK's `groupMaxGuard.wouldExceedGroupMax`?; (8) group-limits (pre-existing): on a raw 1/1 pack slot whose multi-punch max is above 1, the slot sheet's SAVE adds a second hidden pick instead of swapping (`isPackSlotGroup` reads the raw bounds, the engines the effective ones); the seed no longer does (6b7bff4); (9) walk-oos: the tenant's real `get_out_of_stock` rows were never read (a Chrome read needs the user's yes): PAY's check counts only rows whose `partner_name` is exactly "Kiosk", while the PDP's stamp ignores the partner — a partner named otherwise greys the option on the PDP and PAY never flags it; (10) walk-oos (0d745b5): PAY's failing step goes to ErrorOccurred and, in DEV builds only, to `window.__kioskEvents` (the last 50 events) — Rule 4 / C5 forbid console in src; want a sanctioned `console.error` instead? | user / client | ⚠️ open |
| **Out of stock and group limits — known limits (2026-10-14/15, recorded):** (a) the dead rule looks one tier deep (`hasDeadRequiredGroup`, `ponytail:`): a slot whose options all need a dead tier-2 group is not caught; (b) an active, required group with a positive max but no active option is not dead — yet the commit refuses it and the PDP hides it (addon-customize R11's 0 / 774); (c) the buy stage's subCategoryMap fallback (`resolveBuyStageView`) holds RAW entities, so the dead rule never reaches them: the map is filled only while `one_category_at_a_time` is on (the slice defaults to on; useLoaders sets it from the tenant) and read only when the sellable entityMap has nothing for that category, and dead items stay in that map (marked), so in practice nothing changes — the SDK comment in `buyStageUtils.ts` that the map is never populated is wrong (fix it when that file is next edited); (d) the GET side is judged only from the converter's stamps at offer load (`hasUnresolvableGetSide`): an in-stock get item no guest can complete is still offered (the picker drops a dead freebie — oos-2), and a fixed one-size freebie (`isVariantSelected`) is committed without the PDP, dropped only when every size of its base is dead or out; (e) a pack whose groups are all inactive is dropped from the sellable map, so an offer on it stays a locked row with no ADD ITEMS (UB6 F1); (f) an item 86'd mid-session keeps its card and its buy-stage tile until the next menu load (its own outOfStock comes from the menu converted at load); only an OPTION PAY's re-check finds out moves at once, for the items PAY checked — and each PAY replaces PAY's map with its feed for the bag's items, so an option marked out at one PAY reverts to the menu's stamp after a PAY without that item; (g) PAY's re-check skips a tier-2 group the menu does not have (as an unknown tier-1 group) and no longer checks a pick in a group switched off after the add (it is still sent with the order); TB's sized PDP seeds every group of the chosen size, inactive ones too, as empty lists (the commit and PAY skip them; the fork's new-size seed keeps active groups only); LoyaltyRewardsSheet's auto-pick (fork-verbatim) fills a one-option required group whatever its isActive; (h) a split edit (k of N) clears the source row from PAY's two lists while its N − k units keep the old picks: a flagged pick still names itself (the flag rides on the row), a min / max verdict returns at the next PAY (CUSTOMIZABLE since bag-pdp); (i) bag-ribbon: with a XENO reward row in the same bag the bill rounds one sum over both discounts, so a half-penny could land on another row (not observed); (j) pre-existing (the name-layout review): a 32-letter name of wide letters overflows the 844 px name field and runs under the clear button (nothing clips, so the clipping probe misses it) — an ellipsis or a font-size fallback | maintainer | ⚠️ open |

## Engineering notes (learned the hard way — don't relearn)

- **The low-end benchmark (lane perf-bench, 2026-10-16):** `tests/e2e/perf.preview.ts` + `fixtures/perf.ts` on the preview project (a production build, service workers blocked): `TB_E2E_PREVIEW=1 TB_E2E_PORT=<port> npx playwright test perf.preview --workers=1` (~9 min with the build; `perf` alone also matches `boot.preview.ts` when the worktree folder's name contains "perf"). CDP CPU throttling from `PERF_CPU_RATE` (default 6 — the Celeron/Pentium target; it survives navigations), `--disable-gpu` for that file only (software compositing — the worst case, a blocklisted iGPU driver), touch taps at the control's centre after actionability, `waitUntil: 'commit'`. Per (scenario, step), with n and true medians: Event Timing latency grouped by interactionId (no entry ≥ 16 ms counts as < 16), time to the next screen, TBT, long tasks, LoAF with script attribution, frames on the vsync grid (> 25 ms = one or more missed vsyncs — thresholds ON grid points miscount), busy %, heap after GC; every tap names a positive outcome, and idle windows check it every 100 ms instead of sampling frames (the splash counts its video's own frames). The menu mock answers the first getMenu in full and later ones with 304 when the posted MENU_ID matches — production's per-guest path (the Dexie read, then 304) is what gets measured; a 240-card / 40-row LONG MENU gives the grid and rail flings; the 12 MB `../posistKiosk-cx-sdk/StandardMenu.json` (gitignored, local) runs when present. Output: `test-results/perf/perf-<PERF_LABEL>.{json,md}`. Compare runs only on a quiet machine, back-to-back. Playwright wipes `test-results/` at the start of EVERY run in that worktree (a gate's e2e included): copy `perf-<label>.json` out as soon as a run ends. A page whose timers stop (a stalled renderer) hangs `settled()`'s in-page `quiet()` cap until the test timeout — re-run; the 2026-10-16 "final2" first attempt stalled on the splash before any menu fetch, and the re-run was clean. Never compare runs across a Mac sleep (lid closed — caffeinate cannot stop it): the symptom is `net::ERR_NETWORK_IO_SUSPENDED` plus click / setup timeouts, in the benchmark and the gate alike. For a quick A/B of one path, `-g "STANDARD MENU"` (or another test title) runs one test per side in ~1 min.
- **The bill memos are keyed on the bill's inputs (lane bill-memo, M1, 2026-10-16):** `useCartBill` keys on `[cartItems, cartOffer, lockedFreebieAllocation, charges, ctx.tabId, ctx.deploymentInfo, ctx.discountOnAddon, ctx.cartRdx?.cartOffer]` (ctx from `useBillEngine`); `useCartTotal` on the same plus `subTotal` (its catch fallback) and `autoApply` — literal arrays in both hooks (react-hooks/use-memo rejects a shared key helper). Any NEW bill input — a cart field convertCart / settleCart / calculateCharges reads, a setting billCalculation reads, a new context value — must join BOTH keys or the bag shows a stale bill; `billMemo.test.tsx` (13 cart shapes × 26 mixed writes, deep-equal to a fresh computation after each) only catches the inputs it exercises. BagSheet's setAmount mirror now fires once per bill change, not per cart write — fine while BagSheet is the only netAmount writer.
- **Scrollers own an opaque background (lane paint-touch, 2026-10-16):** on a 100 %-scaled Windows panel (DSF 1, ClearType) Chromium composites a scroller only when its own opaque background covers its contents (LCD text needs an opaque layer); a transparent one is repainted on the main thread every scroll frame. One rule in index.css gives `.overflow-y-auto` / `.overflow-x-auto` the tb-surface background; a scroller over another backdrop opts out with `bg-transparent` (paintCss.test.ts pins the rule and the 6 opt-outs). The menu pane, the rail and the buy-stage list opt out ON PURPOSE: any composited layer painted before the multiply-blended menu photos makes Chromium give every photo its own re-sampled layer (option A) — a NEW composited layer under the menu (a scroller, an animation, a video) does the same. Pixel proof on macOS: `--disable-prefer-compositing-to-lcd-text` emulates the kiosk path (macOS cannot render ClearType — an on-device check is owed, P9); Playwright's default comparator threshold (0.2) passed a visibly shorter shadow — judge with threshold 0; the pixel specs live outside the repo (`~/.claude/projects/…/perf-pass/pixel-proof/`). `html { touch-action: pan-x pan-y }` is what blocks pinch-zoom on desktop Chromium (it ignores user-scalable=no); `img { -webkit-user-drag: none }` kills the drag ghost.
- **Closed overlays are shells (lane overlay-shells, 2026-10-16):** OfferRemovalNotice, ProductAddedModal and MakeItAMealPrompt each subscribe to their open flag and render the old component as a body only while open — a hook added to a body costs nothing while closed; an effect that must run while closed belongs in the shell (none today). ProductAddedModal's shell keys on `isOpen`, NOT `shown`: a detour hides the modal without closing it, and the body must survive with its staged picks and its opener (overlayShells.test.tsx pins it under StrictMode). The MIAM pick lives in the shell, so it survives a close (today's behaviour) — and the splash reset (P8).
- **Production freezes only the menu payload roots; the menu cards are memoized (perf wave 1, 2026-10-16):** `frozenRoot` in `useMenuConverters.ts` shallow-freezes each menu-scale root before its dispatch in production, so Immer's autoFreeze stops at it; DEV keeps the deep freeze as the mutation tripwire (unit tests and the e2e dev server still throw on a mutation) — a mutation no DEV suite reaches would edit that guest's menu in production instead of crashing (the `ponytail:`). Every new menu-scale root fetchMenu dispatches must go through `frozenRoot` (the BOOT-1 test checks the slim fixture's roots; its variant and inactive roots are empty). `MenuItemCard` is `memo`, Menu passing its two handlers through a latest-ref (`entityActionRef`, written in useLayoutEffect): a new card prop must be primitive or referentially stable, or every cart write re-renders the grid again (Menu.test.tsx pins it); CompleteYourMealRail's `onAdd` follows the same pattern, its hooks above the early return.
- **The posistKiosk menu settings map (lane menu-subcat, 2026-10-15 — read from the original posistKiosk, the reference):** the live page is `src/pages/Menu.tsx` (routed in `IdleChecksRoutes.tsx`) → MenuLayout → Skin1MenuLayout: the rail `category/CategoryContainerRevamped` beside the pane `menuKiosk/MenuContainerKiosk`; `pages/MenuKiosk/Menu.tsx` (an empty div) and `pages/useMenuLogic.ts` (no importers) are dead code. Settings: (1) `send_categories` — the getMenu payload's `menu.settings`, so PER ORDER TYPE → `menu.entpShowCategory` only when `=== true` (not persisted); the converter builds a FLAT `menu.subcategories` — every sub-category tagged with its parent (`categoryId`, `categoryName`, `categoryAliases`) and sorted by its `order` across the whole menu, not grouped by parent; the rail lists those rows unless `showOnlySupCat || !showEntpCategory` (`CategoryList.tsx:69`; it drops a sub-category whose every item is combo-only), the pane renders one SubCategoryCard each (category cards when the flat list is missing); it also checks the sub-category's scheduler instead of the category's (`menuRenderingChecks.ts`) and hides the accordion header; (2) `one_category_at_a_time` — kiosk settings → `appSettings.oneCategoryAtATime` (any truthy value; the slice default is TRUE, persisted): the pane gets the selected entry alone, a rail tap FILTERS instead of anchor-scrolling, `subCategoryMap` / `categoryMap` are built only while it is on, and a switch resets the pane to its top (`useMenuPaneScrollReset` — the cx-sdk fork lacks it); (3) `show_only_supercategories` — kiosk settings via `useAppSettings.getSuperCategoryOnlySetting()` (no slice field; default false): categories on the rail (big photo tiles, no sub-category rows) and in the pane, whatever `send_categories` says; one at a time, one whole category; (4) `show_big_category_image` — the theme's `ui_settings` (get_theme; `useThemeSettings.isBigCategoryView`, default false) changes only a sub-category row's look: on, a 112×128 photo tile with a centred uppercase 14 px label; off, a 48 px thumbnail beside a left-aligned label — a row never goes without a picture (a placeholder when the sub-category has no kiosk image); (5) the chrome — `full_width_banner` (the banner over the rail and the pane, else inside the pane), `enable_banner_collapes` (sic; it collapses after 10 px of pane scroll), `hide_filter` (drops the tag chips, the Filters pill and LoyaltyHeader above the pane), `hide_veg_non_veg` (the veg / non-veg toggles); (6) `skin_id` "skin_2" = albaik, a horizontal category strip over a 2-column grid that forces the banner and bypasses one at a time — not TB. Not a setting: the order-type name over the rail (TabNameHeader, always on in skin 1). Modes — the rail by `show_only_supercategories` × `send_categories`, the pane by `one_category_at_a_time`: M1 both off — a +/− accordion per category on the rail (one open, its sub-category rows) over every category's sub-category sections; a tap anchor-scrolls and a scroll-spy moves the highlight; M2 one at a time only — the same accordion, ONE sub-category in the pane (wrapped in `categories[0]`, a known trap); opening a header selects its first sub-category; M3 `send_categories` only — flat sub-category rows, every sub-category in that order in one scroll, the same anchor scroll and scroll-spy; M4 both on (the user's screenshot, with the big tiles; the test tenant's values are inferred — no live payload was captured) — flat rows, the selected sub-category alone, the first selected on load, a tap swaps the pane and resets it to the top; `show_only_supercategories` on — category photo tiles, one whole category (one at a time) or every one. The selection and the pane scroll survive /customization (`syncCategoryToRedux`; both reset at the order-type pick). TB since c5d053b (`pages/Menu/index.tsx`, no SDK change): sub-category mode = `entpShowCategory === true && !getSuperCategoryOnlySetting() && Array.isArray(menu.subcategories)`; the rows take only ORDER and membership from the flat list and each sub-category from the sellable, filtered nested `categories` by id — `selectSellableMenu` re-marks only that tree, so rendering the flat list would sell dead items; a gone, empty or repeated id is no row, and each row is wrapped as a one-sub-category entry, so the section renderer and the `rail-<id>` / `section-<id>` test ids are unchanged; one at a time the selection is the SDK's `inViewSubCategoryId` / `inViewSupCategoryId` (cleared by `resetCategorySelection` at the order-type pick), while the scrolling views keep it local; M1 stays TB's view (no accordion, no scroll-spy) and M2 shows one whole category per tap (the sub-category row's decision (b)); `CategoryRail` reveals the selected row by its list's own `scrollTop` — never `scrollIntoView`, which also scrolls the clipped ancestors — at mount and from a ResizeObserver, only when the box hides it. Tests: after a store reset a unit test gets the SDK slice default `oneCategoryAtATime: true`, which a real boot never keeps when the setting is unset — Menu.test's `resetStore()` sets the boot's false; the shared `tests/e2e/fixtures/slim-menu.json` serves `send_categories: false` (its tenant's is true): `menuSubcategories.spec`'s `tenantMenu()` turns it on, and `useMenuConverters.test`'s own `tenantMenu()` keeps it true, so the converter pins are unchanged; one at a time, the spec's `modeMenu()` adds six more copies of each sub-category's cards so the scroll-reset check can fail (a section that never overflows sits at 0 anyway). The SDK's `menuPipeline.convertMenuData` builds the flat list WITHOUT the `order` sort — TB uses `legacyMenuConverters.convertLegacyMenuTree`, which sorts.
- **One rule for which groups count — PAY's re-check and the commit judge the same groups (lane inactive-groups, 2026-10-15):** a modifier group counts only when `isActive` is true; false or a missing key is inactive. Every fresh-session path agrees: the PDP shows (`convertModifiersAccordingly`) and seeds active groups only (the customizable seed skips `!group?.isActive`, b36cf02; tier-2's `buildEmptySelectionsForActiveGroups`; the add-on nested seed), the sized commit skips `!isActive` (`validateVariantSelections`), PAY's re-check skips an inactive group on the row and inside a slot pick, and a group the menu lacks (`outOfStockEngine.checkMinMaxConditionsForGroup`), and the dead rule never calls an inactive group dead. Two exceptions, both open rows: `findFirstMinMaxViolation` and `findTier2MinMaxViolation` never read isActive — they check every key the session holds, so they agree only through the seed, and an Edit (which loads the row's own keys) still counts a group switched off after the add; and OPTIONS — the PDP, both commits and the dead rule read `isActive !== false`, while PAY counts a pick only when `isActive` is truthy (legacy). In tests a group fixture without `isActive` is INACTIVE now (BagSheetStock's min / max cases were re-expressed for it).
- **The dead rule — an item no guest can complete reads out of stock (lanes walk-oos 2, decision 1 and dead-offer, 2026-10-14/15):** ONE SDK rule, `@cx-sdk/catalog/oos/optionStock`: `hasDeadRequiredGroup` — an ACTIVE, REQUIRED group (effective min > 0: the multi-punch min when that setting is on; a missing min reads 0) with no positive effective max, or with every active option out of stock as the PDP judges it (`isOptionOutOfStock`: PAY's feed map over the menu's stamp for addon / combo / vaddon); `markUncompletable(entity, modifiersMap, stockMap, multiPunch)` stamps such an entity `outOfStock: true` — a sized item size by size, the item itself only when no size on offer is left in stock and at least one is dead (sizes only sold out are left alone); nothing dead → the SAME object, so the selectors return the store's own data and a stock change that kills nothing re-renders nothing. It is applied at every entity source, each taking PAY's stock map, the modifiers and the multi-punch setting as selector inputs (re-evaluated when stock moves): `selectMenuSellableEntityMap` (the rails, the added-modal strip, Complete Your Meal, the PDP rail, the rewards rail, buy-stage item and category tiles, Select a Size from a suggestion), `selectSellableMenu` (the cards, quick add, Select a Size, the card tap's PDP, loyalty rewards), `selectSellableVariantObject` (size-keyed buy-stage tiles), `useSellableCombos` (the meal prompt's list AND the classifier's `filterByTags` — one list, so the prompt never opens empty), FreebiePickerSheet (a dead freebie dropped) and `useOfferSavings` → `buildMenuStock` (a dead buy side → the struck row). A new surface that offers an item reads one of these, never the raw `entityMap` / `selectMenu`. One tier deep (`ponytail:`); the known limits row lists what it does not reach.
- **leastValueGrants reads the bill's own lines (lane bag-ribbon, 2026-10-15):** `src/components/cart/leastValueGrants.ts` maps the applied least-value offer onto bag rows by itemId — the units from the SDK's `getLeastValueAllocations(cart)` (orderBuilder: the lock when set, else the cheapest-first split; `annotateCartItemsForLeastValueOffer` calls the same function, so the chip and the bill cannot disagree), the money from `bill._items[i].discounts` by cart index (convertCart maps the rows 1:1 and calculateBill never reorders `_items`). The bill adds the least-value amounts UNROUNDED and rounds the sum once (`getTotalDiscount`), so a row's saving is its rounded step in that running total — rounding each line drifts a cent every few rows; the rows then add up to Discounts to the penny (before taxes and round off, their prices to the Total too). BagItemRow floors a row at £0 (an offer over 100 % — posistKiosk's `Math.max`). Never re-price a bag row in the UI; read the bill. Edge, unobserved: with a XENO reward row in the same bag the bill rounds one sum over both discounts, so a half-penny could land on another row. If the SDK ever exports a per-row annotation, use it.
- **Bounds and units at every commit (group-limits and the integration fixer, 2026-10-15):** `readBound` (`@cx-sdk/ordering/customization/groupCompletion`) — a min / max counts as itself when it is a positive number, else 0 (missing, null, text, negative) — is read by `findFirstMinMaxViolation`, `isGroupMinMaxSatisfied`, `validateVariantSelections` and tier2Logic's `findTier2MinMaxViolation`, and a twin of it by PAY's sweep (catalog never imports ordering); the PDP's `hasNoMax`, the header and the seed read `Number(x) > 0` the same way. It applies at the comparison only, so the legacy logs keep the raw values (log parity). Every commit counts UNITS (summed quantities) on both tiers; the fork's sheet twins in `bottomSheetLogic` still count rows against the raw bounds. The PDP's no-max guard is TB-side (`useCustomization` `hasNoMax` / `refusesAdd`, `openTier2New` — the open calls row (7)). `GroupHeader`: `atOne` shows the counter at a max of 1, `noMax` prints "0 of 0"; the callers pass `atOne` only for stepper rows or several IN-STOCK options that do not swap (e2a3478). The PDP seed, in one place: active groups only, never a sold-out default, defaults up to the raw max and then the effective max, a group with no positive effective max empty, one default in a raw 1/1 slot.
- **SDK mutants without touching the shared checkout (round 3 lanes and fixers, 2026-10-14/15):** a vitest `load` plugin serves a MUTATED COPY of an SDK file at its real path, so bare (`@cx-sdk/…`) and relative importers both see it, while the shared checkout is only read (sha256-checked before and after); controls: the unmutated copy green, a copy that throws on load killed (proof the plugin was live). For an e2e failing-first run or a bundle baseline, the copy-serving vite config must sit where `vite` resolves (e.g. the worktree's `node_modules/.cache`) — run from the scratchpad it fails with ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND; the same plugin under `vite build` measures a lane's exact byte delta. TB files are mutated in place and restored with a `cmp` check. The scripts lived in the scratchpad (lost on reboot — rebuild them from this note).
- **The checkout flow map (lane back-nav, 2026-10-15 — the definitive map):** settings: CRM = `!skip_crm_page` (`shouldSkipCRM` is "hide CRM"), TABLE = `tab_type` "table", LOY = `enable_loyalty` AND the partner (`getIsLoyaltyOn`); the slice flag `skipPhoneAndName` is set by no code and adds or skips nothing. ONE source: `src/hooks/utils/useCheckoutFlow.ts` STEPS /cart, /tent [TABLE], /phone [CRM && !LOY], /customerName [CRM], /payment, /receipt, with `applies()`; `nextCheckoutStep` / `previousCheckoutStep` return only steps that apply, so a BACK from a stale URL never lands on a skipped step. Pre-checkout (unchanged): /start tap → /second (no BACK); /second: a card → LOY ? /phone (pre-menu) : /menu, CANCEL ORDER → /start; pre-menu /phone (LOY only): CONTINUE / SKIP / a failed lookup → /menu, BACK → /second, CANCEL ORDER → /start at once; /menu: VIEW MY BAG → /cart, an item → /customization, CANCEL ORDER → confirm → /start ("NO, BACK TO MENU" stays); the PDP: ADD TO BAG and BACK → `resolveCustomizationReturnPath` (/menu or /cart); /cart (the bag over /menu): PAY → the first step after /cart that applies = the SDK's `resolveCheckoutRoute` (TABLE → /tent; !CRM → /payment; LOY → /customerName; else /phone in checkout mode — pinned equal for all 8 combinations), CLOSE → /menu. Checkout (forward and BACK read the helper): /tent CONFIRM / NO THANKS → the next step, BACK → /cart (the typed number committed; an empty bag → /menu); checkout /phone CONTINUE / SKIP → /customerName (a literal, equal to the helper whenever /phone applies), BACK → TABLE ? /tent : /cart (the number committed — SKIP and an empty CONTINUE commit "" since afd8d06), CANCEL ORDER → confirm ("NO, BACK") → /start; /customerName CONTINUE → /payment (a zero-bill loyalty order pushes → /orderSuccess), BACK → !LOY ? /phone (checkout) : TABLE ? /tent : /cart (the name committed; refused while placingOrder; a return skips the CRM prefill — `usePageEnter`'s "back", from the session trail `notePath` keeps in AppRoutes: re-check the prefill if the trail rules change), order-error BACK TO BAG → /cart, CANCEL ORDER → confirm ("NO, BACK") → /start; /payment PAY AT COUNTER → 3 s window → /receipt (a literal, equal to the helper), a Paytm tile → 3 s window → initiate → /paymentPolling (a failure closes in place), BACK → CRM ? /customerName : TABLE ? /tent : /cart (refused while a type is armed or the initiate runs; an empty bag → /menu); unpaid /receipt (COD) YES / NO → push → /orderSuccess, BACK → /payment (refused while pushing or failed), order-error BACK TO BAG → /cart. Post-payment (no BACK): /paymentPolling paid → /receipt (paid: no BACK → /orderSuccess), customer cancel → /payment, not paid → TRY AGAIN /payment or BACK TO BAG /cart, unknown → FINISH /start, no session → /payment or /menu; /orderSuccess 20 s or START NEW ORDER → /start; the Activity Center closes in place over /start and /LoadingResources; idle → /start from any screen. Per-setting walks (BACK is the exact reverse; PAY AT COUNTER then /receipt): CRM + TABLE + !LOY cart → tent → phone → customerName → payment; CRM + TABLE + LOY cart → tent → customerName → payment; CRM + !TABLE + !LOY cart → phone → customerName → payment; CRM + !TABLE + LOY cart → customerName → payment; !CRM + TABLE cart → tent → payment; !CRM + !TABLE cart → payment. posistKiosk: the same except /payment with CRM hidden on a table order (it goes to /cart; TB to /tent, the user's "previous level" rule) — and its main /payment has no BACK at all (only its unavailable screen does: skipCRM ? /cart : /customerName). A new step, or a setting that adds or skips one, goes into STEPS / `applies()` and the FLOWS table of `useCheckoutFlow.test`; a BACK that hardcodes a checkout target is a bug.
- **A live edit of the shared SDK trips the e2e harness (gates 2026-10-14/15):** every worktree links the ONE `../posistKiosk-cx-sdk` checkout and every running Vite dev server watches it, so a lane saving an SDK file mid-run sends HMR update frames to every other run whose modules import it, and the harness's dev-server-reload check (`fixtures/kiosk.ts`: `"reloads": [ "HMR frame …" ]`) fails tests whose own assertions all passed — the back-nav gate on afd8d06 lost 6 ada.spec tests to two `js-update` frames for `src/components/cart/OrderTypeSwitchFlow.tsx` (the HMR boundary above the SDK's `orderTypeSwitch.ts`, then being edited by another lane), phone-layout's lane sweep 6 the same way, walk-bag-dialogs 6 motion.spec on 2026-10-14; a mid-edit SDK file can also fail `tsc -b` once (phone-layout's validate). Recipe: confirm each failure's only diff is "reloads" and that its path leads to an SDK file another session is editing, then, with the SDK quiet, re-run only those in the same worktree — `TB_E2E_PORT=5375 npx playwright test --last-failed --reporter=line --workers=4` (Playwright keeps `test-results/.last-run.json`); green there = gate green, recorded as environmental. Never loosen the check: it is what catches a real reload. Round 3 added a second symptom: a lane's live SDK edit also changes every other worktree's UNIT results — inactive-groups' rule turned 3 of main's tests red in every worktree still at f5a421c (they encoded the old rule); merge the lane that edits the shared SDK before gating the others, and never "fix" those tests or revert the SDK edit from another lane.
- **Load flakes in the unit suite (2026-10-14/15):** Tier2RemovalConfirm "YES → the PDP total drops the pick's price (no throw) and ADD TO BAG names the emptied slot" and CustomizationPack "SAVE on a pick with a REQUIRED nested group routes to tier-2 NEW" await the lazy tier-2 chunk with `findByTestId`'s default 1 s and time out under load (four lanes and a gate on one machine, load average ≈ 10–25): Tier2RemovalConfirm failed the pay-close gate (`tier2-remove-confirm` not found) — it fails about 1 run in 3 under load (walk-cards-prices: 1 of 3 full runs; alone after that gate it passed 2 of 3) —, CustomizationPack in lanes walk-oos, group-counter and walk-cards-prices (at load ≈ 16 even the base b074b85 failed it 3 of 3; another lane saw 2 of 6 folder runs, and 0 of 3 on main sources); both pass alone and in quiet runs. A failure there alone, with nothing in the change near tier-2, is the flake: re-run it and record it so. The cure is a longer `findBy` timeout or warming the chunk once in `beforeAll` (BagSheetStock does; walk-oos does it for BagSheetLateCheckout, ecb0752, merged 2026-10-15). Also seen under load in round 3 (group-limits' validate): the bidi.test whole-tree scan (its 5 s timeout) and CustomizationAddonCustomize "5 · REMOVE…" (a 500 ms real-timer tap guard lapsing) — both pass alone.
- **Prices, totals and the no-photo bell (lane walk-cards-prices, 2026-10-14/15):** `formatPrice` (src/utils/price.ts) = the SDK's `moduloCalcaledValue` is the ONE price formatter, display only — never in money math or a payload; surfaces posistKiosk prints with `calculationWrapper` / toFixed(2) keep two decimals (bag rows and their "off" badge, the bill, ADD TO BAG and the tier-2 total, the payment screens). `useBillEngine` (src/hooks/cartHooks/useCartBill.ts) is the one place the bill engine's pricing context is built (tab id, deployment info, the add-on discount rule, the live cart's offer; `useOrderHook` delegates to it); the bag uses `useCartBill`, the menu bar and the added modal `useCartTotal` (its own module — inside useCartBill it made an import cycle): the same engine and `billTotal` over the cart as the bag leaves it once it opens — `settleCart`, then the offer `useAutoApplyPreview` hands over (the vetoes live in ONE `pickAutoApply` in useOfferAutoApply.ts: the removal notice, the D3 reward lock, then the SDK's `pickSessionAutoApplyOffer`; outside the bag only the operator-flagged pool is ranked — `isOperatorFlagged` — and a subset of `rankOffers` keeps its order, so the pick is the bag's), settled again; never the slice's `netAmount` (mirrored only while the bag is open); Rule 2: an engine throw falls back to the cart subtotal. The added modal previews ADD TO ORDER by replaying its writes through the cart slice's own reducer (`withPicks`; `pickWrite` shared with `addToOrder`). `useOfferSavings(cart?)` quotes the given cart (the store's when omitted) through `useBillEngine`, so menu surfaces rank without a Router. `NoImageBell` + `.tb-no-image-bell` (one index.css rule, a quoted mask `url()`) is the one no-photo treatment, rendered only where `resolveEntityImage` found no photo; offer plates keep their Figma bell.
- **The bag's settling rules and check 8 (walk-offers-2 + its merge, 2026-10-15):** `@cx-sdk/ordering/offer/offerRevalidation` holds `shouldDropAppliedOffer` (revalidation checks 1–7 + check 8) and `settleCart`; BagSheet's revalidation effect and `useCartTotal` import them, so the bag and the menu TOTAL cannot disagree — never re-derive them in TB. Check 8 prices a bill, so it runs only when the caller injects the engine bound to its pricing context — `shouldDropAppliedOffer(offer, items, { cart, getCalculatedBill })`, `settleCart(cart, getCalculatedBill)`, useBillEngine's — and ESLint fails an engine-less call in src (457f734; the deferred item makes the parameters required). Its money is posistKiosk's `offerQualifyingBill`: the guest's own rows (loyalty and freebie rows filtered out, never subtracted) priced as one bill, the ITEM SUBTOTAL, for every offer (onGrossTotal: the open row); minimum > subtotal drops; no minimum or an unpriceable bill never drops; it runs last, below the empty-cart return (a crash-reload's empty first render is never judged). Revalidation is held while a buy-stage journey or a freebie picker is open (`journeyOpen`) and then judges the bag the journey leaves; a picker that fails to render settles like its X (`PickerFailed` in its ErrorBoundary — the old null fallback held revalidation off for the bag session). `useOfferSavings`' `cartSignature` carries each row's `total_price`, so an Edit that only drops an add-on refreshes the ranking, the notice's "Add X+" gap and auto-apply (without it a min-bill auto-apply offer looped: removed, re-applied after every GOT IT). A buy-stage rollback writes the planner's `finalItems` in snapshot order, read from the live store, once to redux (`setCartItems`) and once to IndexedDB (`replaceIndexedDbCart`).
- **The product-added modal opens on every add (lane walk-flow-ui, 2026-10-14):** `useCartHook.openAddedModal` is the single open path (it consumes `recommendationDetour` and then opens without the strip); `addItemToCart` calls it unless `options.suppressAddedModal`, and `useAddEntityToCart`'s +1 (IncreaseItemQuantityById on a row already in the bag) calls it when the bump lands — TB no longer calls the SDK's `resolveAddItemPresentation` / `hasItemRecommendations` (kept for the fork) and the dead quick-cart dispatch is gone. Any new add surface that confirms by itself (the bag, a rail, a stage) MUST pass `suppressAddedModal` and pin it, as Complete Your Meal, the rewards Suggested rail, the PDP rail and the buy stage are (d2e1812): opened under the open bag (both z-40) the modal waits there, arms the tap guard over the bag and greets /menu with a stale Total. `SelectSizeModal` exports `restoreFocus` (the added modal hands focus back through it). e2e: `pages/order` `addOneTap` asserts the modal and closes it (`dismissAddedModal`).
- **A `<button>` centres its content only when it fits (lane rail-wrap, 2026-10-14):** overflowing content is top-aligned (HTML button layout), so a fixed-height button with one line too many loses its bottom line — clipped by an `overflow-y-auto` parent on the last row, over the next row elsewhere. Grow the row (`min-h-*` + padding) instead of a fixed `h-*`; `flex` without `items-center` would drop the implicit centring too — railLongNames.spec pins label centring (±1 px), heading overflow and the pane's sideways scroll.
- **The motion system (lane `motion`, 2026-10-09; contract D-M1–D-M7):** pure CSS + `usePageEnter` only; press STATE (not motion) may be mirrored by `src/utils/pressFeedback.ts` as `[data-pressed]` (no animation API, nothing waits on it, mouse ignored; its source may write nothing else — pinned by its unit test; motion-v2 MV1) — a guardrail now fails on JS-driven motion (rAF, startViewTransition, element.animate, animationend, framer-motion). Route motion is ENTER-ONLY: the incoming page root gets `data-enter="fwd"|"back"` (latched at mount from the navigation type + the trail AppRoutes notes; POP / reloads / the splash never animate) and its content blocks carry `.tb-enter` (`--i` stagger, capped). NEVER View Transitions (a probe showed taps landing on `<html>` during one) and NEVER a key / remount on a route (H1). Closing is instant everywhere; a tap-to-close scrim never animates; a full-screen status overlay (PleaseWait, the menu fetch) may reveal late (`.tb-reveal-late`: invisible for 300 ms, blocking taps from t = 0 — motion-v2 MV4) — never one that REPLACES a dialog: the dialog's scrim unmounts in the same commit, so a late layer leaves the screen bright and dead to taps for 300 ms (motion-v2 review F1; PleaseWait reads `[aria-modal="true"]` at mount, the menu fetch knows its TRY AGAIN, the order-type switch never reveals late). Tokens live in `src/index.css` (`--tb-dur-*`, `--tb-ease-*`, `--tb-dist-*`, the Figma Bouncy spring via `linear()` behind `@supports`). ONE `prefers-reduced-motion` block: every animation ends in 1 ms WITH its fill (never `animation: none` — the 15 modals' centring lives in the fill) AND every from-state token equals its end state (distances 0, scales 1) — a 1 ms animation still paints its from-keyframe once, and an offset first frame broke geometry reads (ada.spec flaked 5/6). A full-surface button with children needs `tb-no-press`. The functional e2e suite runs with `reducedMotion: "reduce"` (playwright.config.ts); `tests/e2e/motion.spec.ts` is the only guard of real motion. If a kiosk shows no motion at all, check the OS — the Activity Center's "Animations" row shows it (motion-v2 MV9; `matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches`). Install checklist: Windows "Animation effects" / Performance Options "Animate controls and elements inside windows" ON; Android "Remove animations" OFF. An effect that aborts its request in cleanup must not let the aborted request's settle clear shared state: StrictMode's dev re-run does exactly that (the /customerName CRM loader vanished in dev; motion-v2).
- **The e2e harness (P9f, `tests/e2e/fixtures` + `pages`):** every spec imports `test` from `./fixtures/kiosk` — unrouted requests are DENIED, page errors / raw i18n keys / dev-server reloads fail the test. Lessons from wave 1: (1) a `page.route` with a function matcher (the shared mock's catch-all) makes Playwright intercept every request; on a reload some in-flight requests reach the CONTEXT routes already settled, so every context-route handler `.catch()`es its fulfill / abort ("Route is already handled!"); (2) always pass the handler to `page.unroute` — the glob alone also removes the shared mock's route; (3) slim-menu fits both rail sections in the pane, so `toBeInViewport()` cannot prove a rail scroll — assert the section MOVED; (4) to pin a countdown, freeze the clock BEFORE the screen mounts (step 100 ms until visible, then run N−1 s and 1 s); on a running clock a countdown check tolerates up to the expect timeout; (5) the a11y sweep (flows/accessibility) is soft so one run lists every failing state; short windows (tent error, the payment cancel window) are swept under `freezeClock` for targets only, never axe; (6) `PENDING_APP_FIX` entries expire — a fixed target fails the flow until its entry is removed; (7) mutation sandboxes keep backups OUTSIDE the project tree (Tailwind scans `src/` and full-reloads connected pages when any file appears there), wait until the dev server serves the edited module, then open one warm-up page. Wave 2: (8) a body `page.route("**/api/**")` lets EVERY origin through under the harness (page routes beat its context deny — fonts' Archivo walk hid cross-origin and print-agent calls that way); use the shared mock, whose catch-all is origin-scoped — an app-origin-only route breaks wherever `.env` sets `VITE_API_ENDPOINT`; (9) the harness turns the tenant recommendations off (`window.__TB_RECS_URL__ = ""` unless the spec set the seam): with a developer's `.env` `VITE_TENANT_RECOMMENDATIONS_URL`, every boot fetched the S3 file and 77 of 86 group-A tests failed as "denied" on that machine only; (10) in zsh an unquoted `$VAR` holding several spec paths reaches Playwright as ONE argument that matches nothing — the run silently tests only the other paths; use `${=VAR}` or an array; (11) `playwright --list` and the html reporter write `playwright-report/index.html` into the worktree, which makes Vite full-reload connected pages: set `PLAYWRIGHT_HTML_OUTPUT_DIR` to a scratch dir beside any run on the same dev server. Follow-ups (lane `harness-fixes`): (12) a string glob is a regex over the whole URL (`**/api/x` → `^(.*/)api/x$`), so a `**/api/<path>` route answers EVERY origin — the shared mock's named routes, `getServerTime` and `routeOffers` now match through `backendPath` (the same URL matching, backend origins only), `isBackendApi` is exported for a spec's own backend-wide route, and `holdRequests` parks only the backend origins' requests (the app's chunks included); harness.spec's two "DENIED: a cross-origin call …" cases pin it. Still answering any origin: the routes the checkout / paytm / loyalty fixtures register themselves, 151 spec-local `**/api/<path>` overrides in 28 specs, and the visual walks' catch-alls; (13) `mockCheckoutBackend` routes getPipelines only when given `tabType` — before, it replaced a spec's own `pipelines` (globalError CRM / DP CAP en+ar and visual-entry ORDER COMPLETE, LOYALTY (ar) silently ran on its English-only row); harness.spec "HOUSE ORDER" pins both halves; a spec that gives the shared mock no pipelines still routes its own after the checkout mock (secondPipelines, orderTypeSwitch, motion) — their comments state it since residuals-a; (14) `src/index.css` `@source not` keeps `__tests__`, `__mocks__`, `*.{test,spec}.{ts,tsx}` and `setupTests.ts` out of Tailwind's scan — a write to a scanned unit test pushed an `index.css` update into every open e2e page; harness.spec "DEV-SERVER RELOAD (source)" runs the plugin's own compile + Scanner (resolved THROUGH `@tailwindcss/vite`, whose pinned pair they are — not our dependencies) and fails if any unit-test file is scanned. A dev server that was running when this `index.css` landed keeps the old watch entries (each write to a test file the old CSS scanned fully reloads its pages): restart it. Production CSS lost 17 test-only utilities and `--ease-in` (−985 B).
- **The Order Complete countdown fill in e2e (lane success-timer, 2026-10-14):** `.tb-countdown-fill` runs as long as the screen is up (20 s), so the shared `settle()` (`tests/e2e/fixtures/capture.ts`) exempts it by `animationName` — under motion-on it would otherwise wait the whole countdown out (under the suite's reduced motion the fill is not rendered at all); motion.spec M0 counts ONE entering block on Order Complete (2 while the photo panel existed) and reads the fill's run, and M11's ADA-exit check exempts the running fill and asserts its `currentTime` never goes back (the mode switch never restarts it). Chrome quirk: seeking a PAUSED CSS animation to its very end in one task drops it from `getAnimations()` and the compositor keeps the frame it paused on — seek inside the active phase and do the final seek in its own task (only the spec seeks; the app never does).
- **Walking the kiosk in the user's Chrome (2026-10-14, the live walk):** the window would not go portrait (a 1080×1920 resize is refused — "Bounds must be at least 50% within visible screen space" — and smaller sizes reported success but stayed 1060×737, DPR 2), so the stage ran at ≈0.384 (≈414×737) and details were read with zoom. A ref-based click or `scroll_to` runs `scrollIntoView` inside the extension and scrolls the overflow-hidden stage wrapper (`#root > div`) — the whole stage shifts 68–357 px and stays shifted, even back on the splash (a tool artefact: the app's own `focus()` calls never moved it); reset `scrollTop` / `scrollLeft` to 0 and click by coordinates. `read_network_requests` caught no backend XHR — a `PerformanceObserver` on resource timing did (observe; never read the buffer: the dev build fills its 250 entries with Vite modules). Arabic on a tenant without a secondary language: dispatch LanguageSheet's own `setSelectedLanguage` (session-only, never `setLanguages`). A reload clears every in-page helper, and an idle tab hits the inactivity reset.
- **ErrorModal and the guest confirms (walk 2026-10-14, lane walk-bag-dialogs):** `initialFocus?: "primary" | "secondary"` (default primary) — pass "secondary" when the primary IS the consequential choice (delete, switch, replace): Remove item, the order-type switch and the PDP add-on prompts do; error dialogs keep it on TRY AGAIN / GOT IT / KEEP PAYING — except /payment's initiate-failure modal, which passes "secondary" so focus lands on CLOSE: its TRY AGAIN mints new ids and may arm the card machine (pay-close, 2026-10-14); the confirms that are not ErrorModals (tier-2's inline confirm, CancelOrderModal, the staff logout) autofocus their safe choice themselves; IdleGuard ignores focus events, so the autofocus never re-arms idle. Its CTA pair wraps (`min-h-[60px] px-[10px] py-[8px] leading-[20px] [overflow-wrap:anywhere]`, the OptionCard CTA's language): measured in the real Archivo 125 %/900 at 18 px, 258 px is free inside the insets — the widest fixed label, "Yes, replace item", is 237 px, "No, keep Take Away" 255, "No, keep Restaurants Co." 343.7 (it spilled under YES, SWITCH). Measure label widths with the woff2 embedded as a data: URL: Chromium refuses a file:// font from an about:blank page, and the fallback face measures ≈9 % narrower. Still fixed: IdleTimeoutModal's buttons (fixed copy, measured to fit) and CancelOrderModal's (they grow sideways, `min-w`). `KioskNumpad`'s `accepts(next)` is opt-in: a rejected digit renders aria-disabled, opacity-40, without press travel (Edit how many passes `k <= N`; the phone, OTP and tent keypads pass none and still drop digits past `maxLength` silently). A PDP add whose return path is `/cart` passes `suppressAddedModal` (`addTier1CustomizationToMIAMCart`, both paid branches): every bag-origin detour (the Complete-Your-Meal rail's size / options intents, the repeat sheet's NEW CUSTOMIZATIONS) carries direction "cart" and edits never open the modal — opened there, it waited under the bag (both z-40) and greeted /menu with a stale pre-discount Total (a combo-upsell rail item still closes the bag first and returns to /menu, where the modal is in context). Known, pre-existing: no dialog returns focus to its opener on close (the product-added modal does since walk-flow-ui, 4e1120a); non-confirm alertdialogs (OfferRemovalNotice, LoyaltyErrorModal, the order-error panel) move no focus in.
- **Offer quotes after the walk (lane walk-offers, 2026-10-14):** (1) `useOfferSavings.billFor` — the one probe behind the savings, the baseline subtotal that min-bill gaps compare against and `netAfter` — prices the cart WITHOUT the applied item offer's `isGetItem` rows: the bag a swap leaves (every swap sweeps them first, `useOfferApply.clearFreebieRows`); (2) `rankOffersForKiosk(offers, ctx, isOnMenu, addonsDiscounted = false)` prices a COPY: the fixed get list as ONE synthetic entry worth `fixedGetCeiling` (each in-stock entry's dearest unit at its own stamp × its units — a fixed size its own price, an open size choice its dearest active, in-stock, not `isGetVariantDisabled` size; "or" entries are alternatives, "and" entries sum; group-wise counts N units, a fixed entry repeating), every same-or-less ceiling applied (not only beside category groups), an "or" offer as ONE option (the dearest alternative), and — with `enable_dis_addon` on — no figure for a reward built with add-ons; the ORIGINAL offer still leaves the ranker, so routing (`savingFor`), SAVE and the stage are untouched, and item offers' rank-output `requiresChoice` no longer counts entries (no TB reader); (3) "free" is decided from the description's clauses (`buildBuyGetDescription`: every get clause of kind "free"), so the row's line and its description cannot disagree; the picker's title reads its options' stamps (free = a 100 % percent stamp); (4) TB DOES pass `discountOnAddon` to `redeemGetItem` (`discountOnAddonRdx`, from `enable_dis_addon`), and the order then discounts a get item's add-ons; (5) plural copy uses i18next's native families (EN one / other, AR all six): `offers.addNudgeItems` (now also the REWARD REMOVED notice's — `removedReasonItemsOne/Other` deleted), `offers.buyStage.subtitleLeast` (count = the free items, buy = the threshold) / `leastNote` and their `…Discount` twins, `offers.mechLeastValueDiscountOne/Other`, `offers.picker.groupSubtitle` (`freebieSubtitleOr` folded in); new: `offers.mechGetDiscounted`, `freebieTitleDiscount`, `freebieTitleMixed`, `picker.subtitleOption`; (6) offer names take ONE casing, CSS `capitalize` — OfferRemovalNotice splits its sentence at the `{{name}}` slot so the name is its own span (the AR isolates kept); a JS title-case (58e7500) broke 4 e2e specs that read the raw name back; (7) the SDK half lives uncommitted in the shared checkout (the SDK-commit row); its mutants ran from sibling copies through a vitest / Vite alias, never in the shared file (md5-checked after each).
- **Vite 8/Rolldown CJS interop**: default-import of CJS yields the namespace →
  fixed in SDK (`createFilter`, `autoMergeLevel2`); use named imports or `es/` builds.
- **framer-motion is gone (P9f)**: every animation is CSS (rAF froze in
  occluded windows anyway) — one transform owner per element; Tailwind 4
  translate/scale/rotate are separate properties that compose.
- **Tailwind 4 translate classes use the CSS `translate` property** — they STACK
  with keyframe/inline `transform`. One owner per element.
- **Playwright route mocks**: newest-registered wins; always catch-all
  `**/api/**` first or background calls (PWA version report) 401 the session.
- The offers boot call is **`get_cx_valid_offers`** (not `get_cx_offers`).
- **`tb-modal-enter` is ONLY for `left-1/2 top-1/2` centered modals** — its
  keyframe ends at `translate(-50%,-50%)`, so on flex-centered or bottom-anchored
  elements it permanently shifts them half off-screen. Sheets/dialogs use scoped
  position-neutral keyframes (see Tier2CustomizationSheet/SlotSelectionSheet).
- `setSelectedTabId` writes `auth.tab_id` (`selectedTabId` is a dead field, SDK-documented).
- `mandatoryFullscreen` defaults **true**; `selectedLanguage.code` inits to `""` (use `||` not `??`).
- Ports: tacobell dev+e2e = **5373** (strictPort); 5173/5273 belong to posistKiosk.
- Dev backend: `https://test.restroworks.biz` (qrmenu.posist.co is dead — 404s).
- **`appSettings.deploymentInfoSettings` must ALWAYS hold an array.** The SDK's
  `hasSetting(name, settingsArray)` (ordering/order/orderBuilder) does an
  unguarded `.filter` and is reached from `convertOrderItems` → `convertCart` →
  `getCalculatedBill` — i.e. on EVERY bill recompute while the bag is open. When
  P8a joined `getDeploymentInfoApi()` to the boot sequence, storing a degraded
  body (`{}` from an error envelope) made the bag throw on every cart change.
  Normalise at the dispatch, not at the consumers.
- **Two money paths since P8b.** Pay-at-counter = `pushOrder` with
  `payment.paymentType` exactly `"PAY_AT_RESTAURANT"` (or `""` on the zero-bill
  loyalty path): its three-way switch places NO order for NI/Paytm values, so a
  stale type is a success screen for an order that does not exist (directOrder
  clears a leftover Paytm type). Paytm = the BACKEND places the order from the
  `order_details` frozen at initiate, on its webhook or on ANY status read; the
  paid tail is `recordOrderLocally` (bookkeeping, no RTK call), never
  `pushOrder`. The gateway seam `resolveKioskGatewayKind` is still unused in TB.
- **The boot loader deliberately does NOT port `connectTOGeideaSocket` /
  `connectWithNeoLeapSocket`.** Their absence is what keeps the kiosk
  peripheral-free by construction rather than by fixture discipline; the
  payment-settings fetch also filters out the synthetic Mashreq row so the
  terminal bridge can never be probed. Every e2e must additionally abort
  `https://localhost:65505/**` (the print agent is a different origin, so the
  `**/api/**` catch-all misses it) and 500-guard the gateway endpoints so a
  regression fails loudly instead of passing against an empty mock.
- **App-wide overlays mount at the ROUTES level, not inside a page.** MIAM and
  the repeat sheet are opened by `useAddEntityToCart` dispatching redux modal
  state from ANY screen, so a page-local mount makes the tap dead elsewhere and
  leaves the state to surface later on an unrelated screen. They live in
  `AppRoutes`' pathless `InSessionOverlays` layout route (inside ProtectedRoute,
  so never on Registration) — the fork does the same thing in IdleChecksRoutes.
- **recommendationDetour lifecycle (lane added-modal 2026-10-09; staged 2026-10-12):** a UI
  flag in menuSelections, never persisted. SET only by `Menu.handleSuggestionAdd` when a
  strip suggestion opens a chooser (Select a Size, or the PDP via addEntity's
  `onBeforeLeave`); the product-added modal stays OPEN but renders nothing while it is
  set, so its picks (`menuSelections.upsellStaging`) survive the trip. While the modal is
  open AND the flag is set, `useCartHook.addItemToCart` PARKS the finished row
  (`stageConfiguredUpsellItem`, after the DP check, before the cart write / Dexie /
  CartModified) and returns true — the chooser closes as on a real add, no second modal
  opens (one hop), and ADD TO ORDER replays the row. CLEARED by `closeModalStates` (every
  PDP exit: BACK, "−" at 1, ADD TO BAG, a DP refusal), the Select-a-Size X and its
  size-only CONTINUE (parked or refused), the repeat sheet's close, a menu card tap or its
  "+", and the session reset — each clear brings the modal back. BOTH edges arm
  `DetourTapGuard` (src/components/menu, mounted last in InSessionOverlays, so it outlives
  /menu → /customization; its timer is cleared on unmount — never arm it from an effect on
  the PDP): the start puts the chooser under the finger, the end the modal. The old
  consume path (the next added-modal open stamps `suppressRecommendations` and clears the
  flag) is now only a fallback for a detour with the modal closed — unreachable, since the
  modal no longer closes for one. NEVER cleared by an effect: StrictMode's dev re-mount
  would clear it before ADD TO BAG (useCustomizationDetour guards this). ~~The plain-add
  open check reads `selectMenuSellableEntityMap` — the same map and live out-of-stock map
  the strip resolves over through the SDK's `resolveItemRecommendations`~~ No plain-add
  open check since walk-flow-ui (every add its caller does not suppress opens the modal);
  the strip still resolves over `selectMenuSellableEntityMap` and the live out-of-stock
  map through the SDK's `resolveItemRecommendations`
- **The product-added staging (2026-10-12):** `upsellStaging.pending` (one-tap units by
  id; a zero is deleted) and `.pendingCustom` (parked chooser rows) are emptied by EVERY
  `mutateAddToCartModal` — a new add's open, a close, the session reset — so picks never
  outlive one visit. ADD TO ORDER (ProductAddedModal) writes ONE row per product with all
  its units (onto the row the menu "+" bumps — the first paid row of that id — through
  `updateItemCart`, else a new row), then replays the parked rows, unpicking each write
  that landed, so a refusal leaves only what was refused. A parked row replays with
  the itemId it was parked with (`stamped`, a TB-local addItemToCart option):
  restamped in one handler, two rows of one product got the same "<ms>_<id>" key
  and the reducer merged them — a Regular + a Large became 2 × the Regular. That
  relies on
  `useCartHook.isDpMaxQuantityValid` reading the LIVE store: with the render's cart, every
  write in one handler was measured against the cart before the first, and two picks
  passed a cap of one together. Suggestions and a size chosen in Select a Size pass
  `forceNewCustomization` (TB-local `useAddEntityToCart` option: the classifier gets
  `showRepeatCustomization: false`) — the repeat sheet's "+" writes outside
  `addItemToCart`, past the staging. The CTA's label follows `hasPicks` — exactly
  the set addToOrder buys — ADD TO ORDER, else NO THANKS (a strip) / GOT IT (none).
  Every add surface reachable while the modal is hidden must park or be gone: the
  PDP hides its own rail while the detour is set, and Select a Size's CONTINUE into
  the PDP (a size with options) keeps the sheet mounted as the cover until the
  route change unmounts Menu — RR7 navigates inside a transition, so the page under
  a closed sheet stays live until the PDP commits (a Menu-local latch makes a second
  CONTINUE a no-op; the sheet's X resets it)
- **The PDP commit answers three ways (added-modal review R2):**
  `addCustomizationToCart` / `addTier1CustomizationToMIAMCart` return `true`
  (the row landed: ItemAddedToCart is sent), `false` (validation failed: the
  completion warning opens) or `undefined` (the DP cap refused the paid add —
  `addItemToCart` → false — so only its global error shows and no event is
  sent). `handleAddToBag` tests `=== false`; never test truthiness
- **The PDP's size grid after Change (walk 2026-10-14, lane walk-pdp-prices):** two flags, never one — `sizeGridOpen` (the view: the grid shows until a size is confirmed, and again after "<SIZE> · Change", over the current size) and `sizePending` (no size at all: an empty `selectedVariant`, the hook's own commit rule; the pre-size price line, the priceless ADD TO BAG and the completion warning's `sizeMissing` read it). Re-picking the current size only closes the grid: `fetchAddonsForSelectedVariant` always dispatches empty selections, so even the same size wiped its add-ons (posistKiosk resets them — a deliberate divergence). ADD TO BAG with the grid open closes it in `flushSync` first: useCustomization's error AutoScroll measures the ringed group's `offsetTop` synchronously inside the commit, and a queued close left the groups out of the DOM (`scrollTo({ top: NaN })`, the pane at 0, the ring below the fold) — a handler that reveals the groups in the same tick must flush that reveal. `AutoScroll` (`src/utils/autoScroll.ts`) still has no NaN guard (a missing target's `offsetTop` is undefined, and `undefined − 450` scrolls to the top) — add one if a second such path appears. A lone pickable size opens chosen through `pickableSizes()`, the grid's own filter (an S1 `preselectedVariantId` still wins; an out-of-stock lone size can be preselected, as the old one-tile grid allowed). `useLineClamp` (OfferRow's measure, moved verbatim) gives the PDP description its Show more only when clamped — at > 1 px of overflow, so 1 px of headroom (a one-line Arabic description measures 25 vs 24): check it with the kiosk's Arabic fallback font. Testid `pdp-price` (the price line); `RadioMark` (OptionCard) is the shared 36 px radio.
- **Select a Size is mounted only while open** (Menu `{sizeEntity &&
  <SelectSizeModal …/>}`, review R3): a stay-mounted modal kept an abandoned
  pick, which came back ticked with CONTINUE live
- **e2e: ~~a re-tap of an in-cart PLAIN item opens no added modal~~ every menu add opens the added modal (walk-flow-ui, 2026-10-14)** —
  ~~useAddEntityToCart's "add" intent calls IncreaseItemQuantityById, which bumps
  the row silently; a spec that needs the modal twice uses a second plain
  source or a fresh boot~~ the "add" intent's +1 (IncreaseItemQuantityById) opens it
  too, so a spec that adds on the menu asserts the modal and closes it
  (`pages/order` `addOneTap` / `dismissAddedModal`); the bag's steppers and the
  repeat sheet still bump silently. A tap on a suggestion card's photo or name must be a
  `page.mouse` click at that point (the overlay covers the tile, so
  `locator.click` on the image waits forever). After a choice suggestion's tap
  the stage swallows taps for 500 ms (`detour-tap-guard`): `locator.click`
  waits it out, `page.mouse` does not; the bag's CTA bar does the same for 500 ms after the
  bag opens over the menu and after a rewards / picker / buy-stage sheet closes (`bag-cta-tap-guard`; a bag that mounts open, e.g. after the PDP's UPDATE, is not covered).
  Under no-preference, let the card's
  spring settle (`getAnimations()` … `.finished`) before measuring a tile.
  Playwright's `dblclick` sends both clicks before a route change renders the
  next page: on a route-changing surface (the PDP) the second half lands on
  the old screen, while a surface mounted in the same commit (Select a Size,
  and the modal coming back when it closes) takes it — so productAdded P7b,
  not P7, guards DetourTapGuard, both ways (its mount in InSessionOverlays has
  no unit coverage: the route tests render only `/`). Under CDP slow motion
  (`Animation.setPlaybackRate`) an animation already running when the rate
  changes can read a negative currentTime (−8 s) once it is back at 1, and
  settle() waits it out: finish what the test is not about (P8 finishes the
  menu's bag-icon bump behind the modal). Concurrent mutation authors each get their
  OWN sandbox copy, never a shared one: a dev server watching another
  author's live mutants pushes Tailwind `index.css` HMR frames and the harness
  fails on "reloads"
- **An add-on's own picks (lane addon-customize, 2026-10-10 — the posistKiosk flow):** a PDP option card whose item resolves (SDK `customization/addonCustomize.ts` over `findAppropriateVariantForSelectedItem`) to an entity with active, in-stock groups of its own is a TWO-button card: CUSTOMIZE (only once picked) above CHOOSE / REMOVE, the whole group 476. `planAddonTap` mirrors the fork's `planCustomizationSelect`: a REQUIRED item's CHOOSE opens the customize page NEW (nothing reaches tier-1 until UPDATE); an all-optional one is added pre-shaped (`buildNestedEntityToAdd`, active in-stock own groups seeded); CUSTOMIZE / a body tap on a pick stamps the row's itemId if it has none, then opens EDIT; REMOVE asks first when the pick carries own picks; a swap group asks "Replace X in your meal?". MONEY: own picks reach the BILL and the POS only in a `_combo` group under a truthy parent applyAddonsPrice (as `mapComboItems[].addOns`); in `_addons` (and every other suffix) `orderBuilder` drops them — the PDP and the bag line still include them (posistKiosk's behaviour; Q1, S11). Under applyAddonsPrice false or undefined a `_combo` add-on's own picks go to the POS unpriced, while undefined still prices them on the PDP and the bag line (Q2)
- **OptionCard's one-button card (lane addon-cta, 2026-10-09):** every non-stepper
  grid card is ONE button — named by `selectLabel` (the item), `aria-pressed`,
  and DESCRIBED by its price line + CTA spans (`useId` ids; no description when
  the card is named by its text, so nothing is read twice); the CTA
  (`optionCta`: CHOOSE / REMOVE / none on a swap group's pick, per the SDK's
  single-slot rule) is a span inside it, so one tap selects once. `reserveCta`
  keeps a hidden CTA box on a swap pick so nothing moves; `tb-press-hit` is the
  old overlay's counter-scale on a `::before` (it needs the card's `relative`)
  so an edge hold still lands. Only pack slots keep the overlay pattern
  (`cta.onClick`). Two select calls in one tap leave the same state (both start
  from the same render-time selection), so a "once" test counts
  `ModifierSelected`, never the state
- **Overlay z-stack (KioskStage is the stacking context; BagSheet z-40 traps its
  own children):** page overlays 40 · MIAM wrapper **45** · CancelOrder /
  LoyaltyRewards 50 · Repeat wrapper **55** · the detour tap guard **58**
  (InSessionOverlays; transparent and hit-testable for 500 ms after a
  product-added suggestion leaves the modal) · LoyaltyLogin 60 · LoyaltySuccess
  70 · LoyaltyError 80 · GlobalErrorHost **95** (in IdleGuard, figma-polish) · the
  idle prompt 100 · FullscreenGate 99998 (audit-fixes U2) and NetworkStatus
  99999 (both outside the stage). The hoisted
  pair carries explicit wrapper z-indexes because equal-z ties were previously
  resolved by DOM order — 45 lets MIAM clear the bag, 55 preserves every
  ordering the repeat sheet had at its intrinsic z-60. Full-stage wrappers MUST
  be `pointer-events-none` with an inner `pointer-events-auto`, or they swallow
  every tap on the page beneath.
- **Item images come from `aggregator_image[kiosk]`, NEVER `entity.image_url`.**
  `image_url` is the master/HD image and on a real deployment the backend fills
  it with a generic S3 placeholder for most items (85 of 122 in the reference
  menu) — rendering it is why every card showed a grey photo glyph. The kiosk's
  own image lives in `aggregator_image[]` under the entry whose
  `aggreagtorName` (payload typo, sic) is "kiosk"; posistKiosk renders it
  everywhere via `getImageUrlFromAggregator(entity, true)`. Use
  `src/utils/entityImage.ts → resolveEntityImage(entity)` (kiosk png → kiosk
  jpg → non-placeholder `image_url` → null) at EVERY render site and keep the
  `{url && <img/>}` guard so imageless items render nothing, not a placeholder.
  Order-payload code (`useOrderHook`) correctly keeps the raw SDK resolver — it
  must return a string, so the placeholder fallback is right there.
- **Xeno is the DEFAULT loyalty branch** — the only partner discriminator in the
  tree is `partner_name.toLowerCase() === "reelo"`; `"xeno"` is never compared.
  Anything not-reelo (including a missing partner) takes the Xeno path.
- **There is NO OTP at loyalty login.** `/phone` runs `check_loyalty_balance`,
  a pure lookup returning points + redeemables in one body. The 4-digit OTP is
  per-redemption, sent by `authenticate_redemption` and verified by
  `redeem_coupon`. (Reelo's OTP is 6 digits — different flow, not built.)
- **Revoke must run BEFORE any session reset** — `checkAndRevokeLoyaltyReward`
  reads the claimed coupon from its render-time closure, which the reset wipes.
  It is a cross-origin GET to `xeno.in:2223`, so Playwright's `**/api/**`
  catch-all does NOT intercept it — route it explicitly or the fetch hangs
  (no timeout, no AbortController in the fork).
- Loyalty rewards join the menu by **top-level entity `id`** (`coupons[].products[]._id`);
  a reward whose entity lacks a price or image is dropped silently.
- `state.loyalty.claimedCoupon` is deliberately NOT persisted — a reboot mid
  redemption strands the points (fork parity, preserved).
- Gate loyalty on `getIsLoyaltyOn()` (settings AND redux), never the raw
  `isLoyaltyOn` selector — `setLoyaltyPartner` flips the redux flag by itself.
- **PDP no-session guard must navigate via `resolveCustomizationReturnPath(location.state)`**,
  never a hardcoded `/menu`: on edit commits the redux session-close flushes
  before the router transition, the guard re-fires, and a hardcoded target
  stomps the `direction:"cart"` return path (found+fixed in P7a e2e).
- VARIANT cart rows must carry `variantPrice` (billCalculation's
  calculatePriceNew reads it, not `price`) or the bill computes NaN — real
  rows from buildVariantCommitPayload always have it; hand-built test rows must too.
- Edit-from-bag preserves qty>1 (commit builders spread `...selectedEntity`
  AFTER `quantity`, so the redux entity's stepper value wins) — pinned by a
  dedicated unit test; the hook's local quantity state staying 1 is not a bug.
  The same spread re-committed a VARIANT row's OLD size on edit: edits build
  with the SDK's `buildVariantEditCommitPayload` (session fields back on top).
- **`swapCartOffer` stamps NO cart row** — item-offer discounts exist only via
  `redeemGetItem` row stamps. A fixed single-"and" freebie has
  `requiresChoice:false`, so routing it down the atomic swap realises £0;
  the choice-path gate must be the fork's literal `type:"item"+getItems`
  test, not `requiresChoice` (caught by the P7b integrator tracing the bill).
- **`emptyCart` resets `offerRemovalModal`** (session-reset hygiene in the
  reducer) — any flow that triggers the removal notice and then empties the
  cart must snapshot the modal and re-dispatch `openOfferRemovalModal` after.
- Offer UI consumes `selectFilteredOffers` ONLY (converter-resolved); offers
  whose `baseItemId`s miss the menu entityMap silently vanish — e2e mock ids
  must exist in the mocked menu.
- **`/start`'s mount owns the session teardown — never reset-then-navigate
  from a screen with guard effects (hazard H1).** RR7's `BrowserRouter` runs
  `navigate` inside `startTransition`, while redux updates render on a sync
  lane: `resetSession(); navigate("/start")` from `/cart`, `/tent`,
  `/payment` or `/customization` renders the EMPTY store on the OLD
  route first, and their empty-cart / closed-session effects re-navigate to
  `/menu` — the kiosk parks on an empty menu. Exits to the splash only
  navigate; StartScreen's ref-latched mount effect revokes (Xeno) THEN runs
  `resetSession("full")`. Proven by e2e mutation (reset-before-navigate
  reproduced the empty `/menu`).
- **Every await-then-navigate/dispatch continuation needs a mounted-ref guard,
  and long async work holds idle at the SHARED hook** (`useIdleHold(active)`
  in usePayAtCounter / useLoyalty). RTK does not abort a mutation on unmount —
  a late `{data}` still arrives and, unguarded, writes the previous customer's
  points/menu/checkout into the next session (found by the P9a reviewers in
  useLoyalty, useMenuConverters, BagSheet.handlePay, SecondLayout, /phone).
  Holds are React state, never redux (a persisted hold would disable idle
  after a crash-reload) and are capped at 120 s while the transport has no
  timeout.
- **react-idle-timer ignores activity while prompted** — CONTINUE (and the
  backdrop) must call `activate()`; `disabled: true` pauses and re-enabling
  starts a FULL fresh period. Under vitest fake timers it captured the real
  timers at import: call its `createMocks()` after every `vi.useFakeTimers()`.
- **Playwright fake clock** (`page.clock.install()` BEFORE `goto`): never jump
  past the whole idle period in one `fastForward` — that skips the prompt and
  lands straight on the timeout; step 100 s then 20 s, and use `runFor` for
  1 Hz countdowns. Hold a request open by registering a parking route AFTER
