  the fixture's and releasing it with `route.fallback()`.
- React 19 StrictMode in unit tests: wrapping the tree in `<StrictMode>` does
  NOT double-run mount effects — use RTL's `reactStrictMode: true` option.
- **The e2e suite is load-sensitive.** This machine is shared (load average
  often 7–8): under contention the suite runs 2–3× slower (101–119 s vs the
  normal ~40 s) and wall-clock expectations fail — P9a: `checkout.spec` 15 s
  buffer→`/receipt`; P9b: `offers.spec` locators and two `loyalty.spec`
  revoke polls (a 10 s `expect.poll` racing the revoke's own 10 s abort while
  Playwright's route dispatch is starved). Every quiet rerun is green. P9f
  harness work: wait on state, widen polls past in-app budgets, and run the
  gate with fewer workers on a busy box. Unit flakes under load (pre-existing,
  seen by walk round 1): CustomizationPack "SAVE on a pick with a REQUIRED
  nested group routes to tier-2 NEW" (a 1 s findByTestId against the cold
  lazy tier-2 import, ~1 run in 3 on the untouched base) and
  BagSheetLateCheckout "answered while the bag is up" (a 1 s waitFor around
  the PAY fan-out) — both pass alone.
- **Classify timeouts with `isTimeoutError` (`@cx-sdk/core`), never by
  `status === "TIMEOUT_ERROR"` alone.** RTK 2.12 (TB) bounds the WHOLE request:
  a budget that expires mid-body surfaces as `PARSING_ERROR` with
  "TimeoutError: signal timed out"; RTK 2.7 (fork) bounds time-to-headers
  only. TB rethrows RTK errors as `Error(…, { cause })`, so classifiers walk
  `.cause`. A timeout status is a string — never map it to a number (504/505
  de-register the kiosk).
- **A push whose outcome is unknown is never auto-retried**
  (`isOrderPushOutcomeUnknown`: a timeout, or a 2xx whose body was lost — the
  order may exist). Only the customer's Retry resends, with the SAME order id.
- **Programmatic focus counts as idle activity** — react-idle-timer listens to
  `focus`, so an `autoFocus` dialog opening over an abandoned session restarted
  the 120 s clock. IdleGuard passes `DEFAULT_EVENTS` minus `focus` (real
  touches still fire touchstart/mousedown/keydown).
- **Playwright: chained in-app timers need stepping.** A countdown that
  re-arms its 1 s timer from a React effect after each tick (boot retry, push
  backoff) is not reliably driven by one `runFor(N)` under load — step the
  clock in small increments until the expected state appears
  (`stepClockUntil` in recovery.spec.ts).
- **ReachZone must never remount the routes.** It renders
  `{active && <brandZone/>}` then ONE container element at a fixed child
  index; only the container's inline height changes (1920 ↔ 1122). Never a
  conditional wrapper, never `key` on the mode — a remount kills the PDP
  tier-1 session, the OrderSuccess 20 s timer and
  scroll positions. The container uses `contain: layout paint` (the containing
  block for absolute AND fixed descendants, a stacking context, a clip) and
  deliberately NOT `overflow-hidden`: an overflow box is programmatically
  scrollable, so a `scrollIntoView` could slide the whole app inside the zone.
- **Overlay caps are mode-agnostic CSS**: `max-h-[min(<current>,calc(100%_-_96px))]`
  never binds on the 1920 stage and fits the 1122 zone in ADA, so normal mode
  stays pixel-identical and the caps follow the calibration knob.
- **Mutation testing must run in a scratch copy of `src`, never the shared
  tree** — the dev server hot-reloads every edit into running e2e pages (P9c:
  a unit agent's temporary breakages made an e2e run hang 90 s).

- **Splash media (P9d): only `{ media: { home_screen } }` is ever stored.** The
  SDK banner walk, the media merge and `clearMediaDataConvertedItems` are
  guarded (post-P9 25a); banners are still not stored (D1, user 2026-10-06). The e2e `**/api/**` catch-all answers getMedia with
  `{}` — not a getMedia body — so nothing is stored and every spec lands on the
  WELCOME frame unless it routes getMedia AFTER the catch-all. A made-up
  cross-origin host (`https://splash-media.e2e.test`) fulfilled by `page.route`
  stands in for S3 (no DNS needed).
- **Video in tests:** Playwright's Chromium has NO H.264 — e2e video uses the
  593-byte VP8 `tests/e2e/fixtures/splash.webm` (ffmpeg command in the
  splash.spec header). jsdom has no `play/pause/load` (stub the prototype) and
  its `DOMException` is another realm (`instanceof Error` is false there, not
  in Chromium). An undecodable video track beside decodable audio (HEVC/ProRes
  + AAC) plays audio-only with no error, so the splash checks `videoWidth` at
  `loadedmetadata`; a hidden page pauses video, so the stall watchdog never
  counts hidden time.
- **Module-level rate limiters leak across tests in one file** (SplashMedia's
  report map, safeVideoPlay's budget): use distinct URLs or `vi.resetModules()`
  + a dynamic import. Vitest 5 fakes `performance.now` by default.
- **Tests are type-checked (P9f).** `tsc -b` (yarn type-check, yarn build, CI)
  covers unit tests through `tsconfig.test.json` and e2e through
  `tsconfig.e2e.json` — a test or spec type error fails the build. Include
  globs need `/**/*` (TS5010 rejects a trailing `**`), `exclude: []` must be
  explicit (or the inherited app exclude drops every test), and each project
  owns its tsbuildinfo under node_modules/.tmp.
- **Workbox regex routes match a cross-origin URL only at index 0**, so the old
  same-origin regex never cached S3 media. The image route matches
  `({ request }) => request.destination === "image"` (StaleWhileRevalidate:
  it stores opaque responses and repairs a cached error on the next load).
- **e2e mutation sandbox recipe:** rsync the repo (no node_modules/dist/.git),
  symlink `node_modules`, symlink `posistKiosk-cx-sdk` BESIDE the copy (the
  tsconfig references are relative), give the copy its own Vite `cacheDir`,
  serve it on 5399 and point a scratch Playwright config's `baseURL` there.

- **The boot commits atomically (P9e).** `LoadResourcesInitially` stages every
  write and commits them in one synchronous burst after the last await, then
  stamps `autoUpdate.lastBootAt`; a failed boot (normal or refresh) writes
  NOTHING. Any new boot step must stage-and-commit too — an inline dispatch
  reopens the mixed-state bug (empty pipelines, `deploymentInfoSettings = []`
  or payment settings wiped by a blip during a successful boot). The theme is
  the one accepted non-atomic write. The boot no longer wipes the Dexie menu
  cache (freshness = the server's `MENU_ID` / 304).
- **`lastBootAt` is the only boot-age source; a relaunch never boots.** A token
  relaunch lands on `/start`, so both stamps (`lastBootAt`,
  `lastRefreshFailedAt`) are persisted. A never-stamped store counts as STALE:
  any test that idles on `/start` ≥15 s must seed `autoUpdate/setLastBootAt`
  (via `window.__kioskStore` after rehydration) or it will refresh. Refresh
  mode never retries — a failure returns straight to `/start`.
- **Firebase only through the lazy `src/hooks/firebase/fcmRuntime.ts` chunk.**
  `chunkRecovery` skips failures matching `/fcmRuntime/` (a failed FCM chunk
  must never reload the kiosk mid-order); renaming the file silently restores
  the reload. FCM never calls `Notification.requestPermission`.
- **D2 is keyed by RTK endpoint name** (`BACKGROUND_TELEMETRY_ENDPOINTS`, with a
  `satisfies keyof autoUpdateApi.endpoints` guard); the fork passes nothing.
- **Workflow agents all stalling at once = an API/network outage, not the
  code** (2026-10-05 20:40 → after 23:37: every retry produced only its
  opening setup). Recover from the workflow journal and the agent transcripts;
  completed agents' work is already in the tree.
- **Parallel worktree lanes:** a lane is a `git worktree` created BESIDE this
  repo (`../tb-<lane>`) so `link:../posistKiosk-cx-sdk/packages/*` resolves;
  it needs its own `yarn install` and runs e2e with `TB_E2E_PORT=537x`
  (`tests/e2e/fixtures/origin.ts`; never 5173/5273). All lanes share ONE SDK
  checkout: SDK edits happen only in a lane's build stage, additive, in files
  no other lane touches — every lane's dev server hot-reloads them.
  The main checkout's dev server too — and a full e2e run counts the hot
  update as a dev-server reload: walk round 1's bag-dialogs run lost 6
  motion.spec tests to walk-offers' SDK edit (14:05), 24/24 alone. Re-run a
  tripped spec before reading it as a regression (2026-10-14).

- **Arabic runs are FSI…PDI-isolated (P9f)** by the i18n post-processor
  (`bidiIsolate`) plus the interpolation `escape` hook in `src/i18n/index.ts`:
  in an RTL language every translated string and every interpolated value is
  a first-strong isolate, so numbers, prices and Latin names inside Arabic
  stay LTR; English is byte-identical. Assert Arabic via `i18n.t()` (exact
  matchers compare the wrapped value) or substrings — never literal Arabic in
  an exact matcher, never a substring that spans a {{placeholder}}.
  An all-placeholder Arabic template resolves LTR (no strong character) and an
  Arabic value then reads backwards: open it with U+200F as the JSON escape
  `\u200f`, never a literal bidi character (`loyalty.pointsCostAlias`).
- **Never `dir` on a flex/grid container** (rows, grids, `justify-between`,
  `start-*` and scroll origins mirror). `dir="auto"` goes only on wrapping
  leaf text (+ `text-start` under a physical `text-left`); today the offer
  row's second line and nudge, and the PDP description. Caveat: HTML
  `dir="auto"` takes the first strong character ANYWHERE in the leaf,
  including inside value isolates, so Arabic
  copy that starts with a Latin {{value}} (offers.removedBody) resolves LTR.
- **An RTL currency symbol rides between LRMs (walk 2026-10-14, lane walk-pdp-prices):** the tenant's "﷼" (U+FDFC) is bidi class AL — inside a price run the digits after it became Arabic numbers and the run reordered ("﷼100 | 254 Cal" painted "254 | 100﷼ Cal", "TOTAL: 169.50﷼"), and as the first strong character of a `dir="auto"` leaf it turned the leaf RTL ("off your order 200.00﷼"). An LRI…PDI isolate is NOT enough: Chromium's `dir="auto"` (HTML's first-strong rule) does not skip isolates, so an isolated ﷼ still turned those leaves RTL. `ltrCurrencySymbol` (`src/hooks/utils/useCurrencySymbol.ts`, the ONE place) wraps a symbol that holds an RTL character in two LRMs (U+200E); LTR symbols (£ ₹ $ € ₪ AED) come back byte-identical and an anchored one unchanged; `useCurrencySymbol` (every price's reader) and `getCurrency()`'s text branch go through it — the SDK builds no currency strings. In Arabic copy the value's FSI isolate resolves LTR on the LRM, so a rial price stays one LTR unit ("﷼17"). The DOM text was right all along: test the PAINTED order — `glyphX` (`tests/e2e/fixtures/glyphs.ts`, shared by currency.spec and menu-arabic.spec) reads glyph positions.
- **PostHog loads lazily (P9f):** `startAnalytics()` right after
  `setAnalyticsPort()`; the kill switch is compile-time (off = not shipped,
  never fetched); pre-load calls queue (cap 100) and replay in order; any
  failure = analytics off for the page load. `posthogRuntime.ts` is the only
  importer and chunkRecovery exempts `/fcmRuntime|posthogRuntime|paytmRuntime/` (renaming
  any of them restores the reload). Load optional lazy chunks with
  `await import()`, NEVER `import().then(handler)`: Vite moves a chained
  `.then` into `__vitePreload`, so a failed chunk skips the handler and a
  handler throw becomes `vite:preloadError` → reload.
- **The bundle gate budgets the BOOT PATH** (`scripts/check-bundle-size.mjs`):
  the module script in `dist/index.html` plus every `<link rel="modulepreload">`
  chunk, gzip level 9, KiB = 1024 B; TOTAL = page JS incl. lazy chunks, SW
  scripts excluded and listed. The ENTRY budget = ceil5(measured +
  10) = 355 KiB, raised to 360 by the user on 2026-10-14 (the CI entry hit
  355.7 after the walk fixes) and to 365 on 2026-10-15 (360.3 after the
  sub-category view, f7bcf56); both times the user chose the raise over the
  SDK lodash trim — only with user approval; every bad input fails closed.
- **e2e: `page.waitForTimeout` is lint-banned under tests/e2e.** Wait on state
  (expect / expect.poll / waitForResponse) or drive time with `page.clock`;
  expect.timeout is 10 s. To prove a negative over a window, sample with
  `expect.poll` until the window closes (fcm.spec E10). Long-press: keep the
  mouse down until the result is visible. After an RTK request, poll the
  store's `api.mutations` until the entries are `rejected` (fcm.spec E12).
- **Overlay-button pattern (P9f)** for a clickable card with a nested control:
  the container keeps the testid and `relative`, no onClick; a named
  `<button type="button" class="absolute inset-0">` opens it and goes LAST in
  the DOM (a later stacking-context sibling would paint above it and leave a
  dead tap zone); the nested control is `relative z-10`. jsdom tests click the
  overlay by role; e2e clicks the container centre or `getByRole("button",
  {name})`. Playwright refuses to click under `aria-disabled="true"`.

- **Names are resolved at RENDER** (`src/hooks/utils/useLocalized.ts`: name /
  description / pipelineName / text) — never write a result into cart/order
  state, dispatch payloads, addEntity args, analytics, React keys or Dexie;
  the push stays primary-language. In RTL they are FSI…PDI-isolated like i18n
  values; descriptions are a shrink-to-fit (`w-fit`) `dir="auto"` leaf.
- **The tenant recommendation map lives in Dexie + memory, never
  redux-persist** (the store is ONE `persist:root` re-serialised on every
  change), and its S3 fetch never uses the RTK transport (it would send the
  device auth headers): bounded, bound to its source URL, last good map kept.

- **ONE lazy bag chunk (offers lane):** the bag's lazy surfaces (the Complete-
  Your-Meal rail, BuyStageSheet, the celebration) share
  `src/components/cart/bagLazyParts.ts`. A separate `React.lazy` import makes
  Rolldown hoist their shared code into an extra chunk that loads at startup
  (measured +3.7 KiB on the boot path) — new lazy bag parts go into
  bagLazyParts (since P8b it holds all lazy UI — see "ONE lazy UI chunk").
- **Offer get entries: the item id is `baseItemId || _id`.** Non-cluster
  offers arrive with `baseItemId: null` and the item id in `_id` (only
  cluster-synced offers fill baseItemId); match rows exactly like the
  converter (`matchesGetItemEntry` in the SDK's offerCommitRules). Live
  group-wise get entries are "or"-only with `quantity: null` and no per-item
  value — the commit must carry the picked units or it grants £0.
- **BagSheet's empty-cart exit** fires only after the bag has held rows while
  open, or once AppRoutes' Dexie sync has settled (`CartRehydratedContext`);
  revalidation skips an empty cart — so a reload on /cart keeps the bag,
  rows and the applied reward.

- **Paytm ids are stored and `await persistor.flush()`ed BEFORE the initiate**
  (redux-persist writes on a timer — the request left ~11 ms before its ids hit
  disk) and re-flushed when a refusal clears them. A reload (`PaytmResumeGuard`:
  decided once, navigate-only — loop-free only while every /paymentPolling →
  /payment exit empties the slice and the paid tail clears the ids before
  /receipt) and an outcome-unknown initiate (timeout, lost body, FETCH_ERROR,
  ≥500) both settle by status with the SAME ids.
- **The Paytm receipt question follows the payment (2026-10-13).** The initiate
  runs at the END of /payment's 3 s window, from the timer's closure: (a)
  `usePaytmCheckout` reads `getPushOrderData` through a latest ref (the tap's
  render still saw type "", so the H-c guard would refuse every first
  initiate); (b) a timer that STARTS money kills its dialog's controls the
  moment it fires — until its batch commits CANCEL / BACK still run the window
  render's closures (PaymentSelection refuses on `bufferTimer` / `armedType`);
  (c) the paid tail is record → clear the ids → flush → navigate /receipt,
  never clear before the bookkeeping (a reload under the /paymentPolling URL
  must still find the session open), and the flush dispatches redux-persist's
  FLUSH action — never `persistor` imported into the lazy `paytmRuntime` chunk
  (the store pulls the `@cx-sdk/core` barrel onto the boot path, +5.9 KiB
  gzip-9); (d) `paymentType` stays: it is /receipt's post-payment signal (the
  PERSISTED type — a money-relevant mode never comes from router state) and the
  ticket's Payment Mode; (e) a paid /receipt whose local record is not THIS
  order's (`order.currentOrder` is memory-only and the session reset keeps the
  last one) renders nothing and goes on as NO — YES could not print.
- **Double-tap fall-through is guarded where a surface closes or a screen
  arrives under the finger:** a transparent, hit-testable layer swallows taps
  for 500 ms (ReachZone's exit guard, the bag CTA, the PDP tier-2 close, the
  detour, the offer tier host, and /payment on arrival and after CANCEL / PAY
  ANOTHER WAY — its tiles start money 3 s later). In e2e a paused clock keeps a
  guard up and Playwright's click waits on it: `freezeClock`'s +1 s jump clears
  one armed before it; after a close on a frozen clock step 500 ms first; a
  real double tap is two `page.mouse.click`s, the second after the screen under
  it changed (`doubleTap`'s same-task pair never sees that).
- **A Paytm status read answers for the phase it was SENT in:** entering
  cancelling / settling clears the reducer's `lastPoll` and bumps the hook's read
  epoch (the deadline inside the 1 Hz tick task, not a passive effect); stale
  answers drop unless "paid" (paid always wins; a paid read stops all reads).
  The one EDC void needs a FRESH "pending" — `classifyPaytmKioskPoll` makes
  `{ error }` and non-envelopes "error", never "pending".
- **`/start` order:** Xeno revoke → `releasePaytmSession()` (bodies built first;
  one status read; EDC void only on a fresh "pending"; fire-and-forget) →
  `resetSession("full")`, which clears every Paytm field, on disk too.
- **D3:** `RECOVERY_EXEMPT_ENDPOINTS` = telemetry + `PAYMENT_GATEWAY_ENDPOINTS`
  (service typed `any` → transportTimeout.test guards the names); status-less EDC
  errors stay unreachable only while those endpoints have no `transformResponse`.
- **The Paytm screen is the lazy `paytmRuntime` chunk** (stable name; chunkRecovery
  exempts it). A failed `import()` stays failed for the page's life, so the
  checkout awaits `loadPaytmScreen()` BEFORE any id and refuses without it (the
  next splash reloads the page). No React.lazy + Suspense: its 300 ms reveal timer
  never fires under a frozen Playwright clock. Lazy code imports SDK subpaths,
  never the `@cx-sdk/core` barrel (Rolldown hoists a vendor chunk onto the boot
  path). `react-qr-code` is CJS: the NAMED `{ QRCode }` + its d.ts, static.
- **Browser Back trap (AppRoutes):** React 19 renders a popstate transition
  synchronously and `window` runs popstate listeners in registration order, so
  the trap is a `useLayoutEffect` below BrowserRouter (ahead of its listener): a
  TRUSTED pop is `stopImmediatePropagation()`ed and the entry re-pushed; script
  pops pass. The old trap never worked (Back reopened /receipt: a second initiate
  or placeOrder). Never `navigate(-1)`; checkout.spec BROWSER BACK guards it.
- **`overflow-clip`, never `overflow-hidden`, on a root that can be 1122 px
  (ADA) with larger transformed children:** a hidden root is a scroll container
  that focus / `locator.click` scrolls (399 px) and never scrolls back.
- **Test seams:** `window.__kioskStore` is DEV-only (production-build e2e asserts
  network counts + DOM); dev StrictMode aborts the print fetch before
  `page.route` sees it — observe it with `page.on('request')`; step vitest fake
  timers in ≤1 s acts (React runs no effects inside one act).
- **ONE lazy UI chunk, operator UI included (P8b merge 2):** `bagLazyParts.ts`
  also carries RewardsSheet, FreebiePickerSheet and the Activity Center's
  ActivityModal (merging offers + P8b put the CI boot path at 359.5 KiB; these
  cuts made it 352.7). ANY extra lazy chunk, even a tiny one, makes Rolldown
  split the boot path into preloaded chunks (+~5 KiB measured) — new lazy UI
  joins bagLazyParts. Until its code arrives a lazy surface shows its own named
  scrim that closes on tap (plain state + `await import()`, no Suspense).
  Since bag-pdp + loyalty-visual: 12 exports, loaded by BagSheet, the Activity
  Center, the PDP and the menu (its always-mounted Xeno rewards sheet, so the
  chunk loads at /menu entry; the scrim fallback reads the open flag itself —
  Menu must not); still ONE chunk. figma-polish: 13 exports — + the tier-2
  CUSTOMIZE page (with the SDK `tier2Logic` only it imports), off the boot path.
- **A failed lazy load sticks for the page's life, so it schedules the healing
  reload:** `loadPaytmScreen` / `loadActivityModal` dispatch
  `initiateWholeAppUpdate()` on a null load and the splash reloads at its next
  dwell. Its old residual (a failed chunk on a page under 60 s old put the
  bag on the crash screen) is gone since figma-polish G2 — see below.
- **No reload cuts the splash's Paytm release:** `holdReloadsUntil`
  (chunkRecovery) makes chunk recovery and `reloadTo` (whole-app update, crash
  recovery) wait for it, capped at 20 s (Rule 2). The hold is module state — a
  test that hangs a release gets its own file. Logout and lost-login redirects
  are not held.
- **Testing Library `findBy*` hangs under vitest fake timers:** await the loader
  inside `act()` instead.

- **Fonts: ONE OFL `Archivo` variable family (wght 100–900 × wdth 62–125),
  widths by `font-stretch`** — body 100 %, `.tb-compressed` 62 %/700,
  `.tb-display` 125 %/900, both classes in `@layer components` so a
  same-element utility wins. Keep each `@font-face`'s `font-stretch: 62% 125%`
  (Chromium infers it; other engines may pin 100 %). The `font` shorthand
  takes stretch KEYWORDS only (`"extra-condensed 700 30px Archivo"`; a `62%`
  throws); `document.fonts.check()` passes a family with no faces. Verify
  font files by decoding fvar/cmap (`fonts.test.ts`), never by name.
- **Quote assets in a CSS `url()`:** Vite inlines small SVGs as data URIs
  carrying `'` `(` `)`; `url(${asset})` is then invalid (a mask paints a solid
  square). Write `url("${asset}")`.
- **Typography/layout regression net:** the capture-only walks visual-entry,
  -order, -offers, -payment (EN / AR / ADA) run `fixtures/clippedText.ts` soft
  after every shot; rerun them for any font, size or layout change (an intended
  crop = line-clamp / ellipsis or a positioned badge). CI: visual-order's
  photo-shrink checks need the S3 photos, and two kills are Arabic-only (the
  Linux OS Arabic font differs).
- **A missing `public/` file answers 200 with index.html** (SPA fallback):
  assert the content, never the status.
- **One Playwright run per dev-server port at a time:** the run that started
  the server stops it on exit and kills the rest (ERR_CONNECTION_REFUSED).

- **Order-type switch = stage, then ONE burst (bag-pdp).** The charges and menu
  fetchers route every dispatch / localStorage write through `{apply, mirror}`
  (default dispatch; byte-identical without, pinned); `useOrderTypeSwitch`
  commits after the last await in one synchronous burst (selection, staged
  charges + menu, the final `pushCharges`, `setCartItems`, `setTent("")`) and a
  failure writes nothing. Never call those fetchers un-staged mid-order; a size
  row's own `variants` is money, re-priced too. Since walk-oos (2026-10-14/15) the
  executor passes the SDK's opt-in `keepSoldOutPicks`: a pick the tab lists and
  switches on but has out of stock stays in its row, priced by the tab and
  flagged (the row names it, Edit drops it, PAY blocks); one the tab no longer
  lists or switches off still removes the row; a reward is still reversed;
  every row the tab serves sheds the old tab's flags (`freshStockFlags`, a kept
  reward's picks too), and the burst clears PAY's feed map and both PAY lists
  (`setOutOfStockItems({})`, `setUpdatedBaseItems`, `setMinMaxErroredBaseItems`)
  before `setCartItems` — stock is per tab.
- **A split edit is ONE write:** planned on `store.getState()` at commit time
  (`planSplitEditCommit`), committed as one `setCartItems` + one Dexie replace.
- **Lazy-only copy lives in `src/i18n/locales/{en,ar}/lazy.json`**, registered
  by `src/i18n/lazyCopy.ts` from lazy modules only (never shadow a boot key).
- **Never dispatch a live bill or engine object into redux:** Immer freezes
  what the store holds and the memoised bill writes its own rows on the next
  render (PAY threw on any deployment with a charge — now `structuredClone`).
- **D3 + F1 live in the new SDK file `ordering/src/loyalty/loyaltyOfferRules.ts`**
  (by subpath): `isOfferLockedByLoyaltyReward`, `getBillLoyaltyDiscount` — the
  Xeno share of a bill is found ONLY by orderBuilder's "Loyalty Item" comment.

- **The global error has ONE renderer, `GlobalErrorHost`** (IdleGuard, after the
  Outlet, z-[95]: above every overlay and the bag, below the idle prompt).
  Dispatch `setShowErrorModalGlobal` with a translated string; never render it
  page-locally or over the PDP completion warning (`useCustomization` raises
  min/max only `if (customization)`).
- **chunkRecovery never reloads mid-order (G2):** once its 60 s loop guard
  holds a reload back the page latches (`healing`); every later failure calls
  the healer main.tsx registers after install (the whole-app update, applied at
  the splash) — a failed module stays failed and the PDP re-imports per mount.
  Every lazy part has a local boundary; tier-2 lives in bagLazyParts (mounted
  only while `MIAMTier2Open`); the PDP loads its parts at MOUNT and hands
  `useCustomization` the planner (the hook importing the chunk = a cycle).
- **Menu charges follow the fetched pipeline's `tab_type`** (the fork keeps the
  stale getter). **A group max counts items on add** (`applySinglePunchSelection`)
  **but units at commit** and in "n of m" — the queued Snacks defect; fix once.
  ✅ Fixed once (audit-fixes UB2): `@cx-sdk/ordering/customization/groupMaxGuard`
  refuses a tap / "+" in units, like the commit, before the engine runs.
  Units at both commits since group-limits (2026-10-15): the sized commit
  (`validateVariantSelections`) and tier-2's `isGroupMinMaxSatisfied` counted rows.

- **PAY re-checks stock (audit-fixes A1):** `handlePay` runs
  `@cx-sdk/catalog/oos/checkoutStockSweep`, loaded through `bagLazyParts` (the
  838-line engine stays off the boot path), and fails OPEN — a failed chunk or
  feed, a non-array feed or any throw sends `ErrorOccurred`
  `checkout_stock_sweep` and PAY takes today's path. It writes ONCE behind a
  CAS: when the live cart no longer `sameCartRows` the pre-sweep rows, nothing
  is written and the guest taps PAY again. The engine MUTATES its input
  (`outOfStock = true` on a dropped pick): feed it the scheduler's
  structuredClone'd rows, never frozen redux rows (that write throws and the
  sweep silently fails open). Only feed rows with
  `partners.partner_name === "Kiosk"` and `inStock === false` count — e2e OOS
  fixtures must send `partner_name: "Kiosk"`. **Since walk-oos (2026-10-14/15):**
  fail-open only past what no check has flagged — with no sweep, the flags the
  LIVE rows carry (`soldOutNames`: the item, its size, or a flagged pick at any
  depth) feed the same decision, so PAY blocks with the named notice; a pick the
  sweep drops is kept in its row, flagged (`payStock.planStockWrite` → SDK
  `sweptPicks.keepSweptPicksFlagged`, `total_price` recomputed the engine's way
  so the row agrees with the bill); Edit deselects it and `releasePayVerdicts`
  drops the row from PAY's two lists in both commit branches; a flag the next
  PAY finds back in stock clears in that PAY's one write (`clearStockFlags`,
  picks too). The server clock never fails PAY (the device clock stands in,
  reported `stage: "server_time"`); every other step reports `ErrorOccurred
  {error_source: "checkout", stage: entry | menu | scheduler | write_back |
  decision | route}` (the sweep keeps its own `checkout_stock_sweep`), and DEV
  builds keep the last 50 events on `window.__kioskEvents`. The min / max scan
  reads bounds through a `readBound` twin and skips inactive groups (the notes
  at the top). e2e: a real preflight failure is `pages/order`
  `breakPayPreflight` (a failed clock no longer fails PAY); unit files that PAY
  through BagSheet warm `bagLazyParts` in `beforeAll`.
- **The fullscreen gate (`FullscreenGate`, U2)** is a portal at z-[99998]
  behind its own ErrorBoundary. Locked = an element in fullscreen, OR
  display-mode fullscreen, OR a window that fills its screen within
  `FULLSCREEN_FILL_TOLERANCE_PX` (2 px, the `ponytail:` calibration knob —
  Chrome `--kiosk` and F11 leave `fullscreenElement` null). Every Playwright
  project sets `contextOptions.screen` 1080×1920 (a top-level `screen` is not
  a test option), so no spec trips it; a spec that wants the gate sets a
  taller screen (fullscreen.spec: 1080×2100). The rulebook's Claude-in-Chrome
  snippet (a `div`, z ≥ 999999, /fullscreen/ text) does NOT match it: TB's
  gate is a `<button data-testid="fullscreen-gate">` reading "TAP TO
  CONTINUE"; one tap enters fullscreen or, refused, takes the gate down for
  the page load.
- **One analytics session per guest (B3):** `resetAnalyticsSession()` (the
  splash tap) runs `posthog.reset()` and re-identifies
  with the six fields `identifyKiosk` last sent, through the same queue and
  kill switch. MenuViewed fires once per Menu mount and never under the open
  bag: /cart's `<Menu bagOpen />` is the same instance as /menu, so the event
  waits for the bag to close.
- **Moved into the SDK (C1), resolved from TB (C2):** bill charges are read
  back from redux through `@cx-sdk/catalog/menu/billCharges`
  `composeBillCharges` (each site keeps its order — the charges call
  menu-first, the menu fetch and the order-type switch deployment-first); the
  localStorage `menuCharges` / `deploymentCharges` keys are write-only mirrors
  (fork parity) — never read them back (a logout purge clears redux, not
  them). `convertOffersForMenu` lives in
  `@cx-sdk/ordering/offer/offerMenuConversion` (goldens:
  `src/__tests__/offerMenuConversion.golden.test.ts`). `resolve.dedupe` (vite
  AND vitest) also covers dayjs / lodash / redux-persist-transform-filter, so
  they resolve from TB's lockfile — keep them declared in package.json;
  `tsc -b` still type-checks the SDK against its own checkout, so its
  `yarn install` stays a prerequisite (README).
- **No console, the COD rule, persistence (C5 / A2 / C3 / B1):** ESLint
  `no-console` covers `src` (tests exempt) — a failure a guest or operator
  should know about goes to `ErrorOccurred`, a best-effort mirror swallows its
  own. The COD rule is the fork's LIVE rule (SDK `checkIfCODAvailable`): the
  `disable_cod_kiosk` row by name or label, the session tab by `tabType` (the
  take-out spellings as one family), COD off only when that tab's
  `selected === true`; PaymentSelection keeps its fail-open wrapper.
  `kioskOpenStatus` persists through a manifest (`pipelinesStatuses`) — never
  neverPersist it: the bag's order-type switch reads it after a crash-reload.
  Only the Paytm payment rows reach device storage, whole (the boot stage's
  `toPaytmKind` filter; D11).

## Session log

- **2026-10-16 (the low-end perf pass, wave 2 + a cold-path fix: the bill runs only on money, closed overlays mount no hooks, scroll lists composite, pinch-zoom blocked — lanes bill-memo, overlay-shells, paint-touch, cold-yield merged; before/after benchmark)** · (1) bill-memo (c7a10a5, M1 — user-approved; billCalculation.js untouched): useCartBill / useCartTotal keyed on the bill's inputs (engineering note); bag "+" 5 → 3 engine runs (8 → 5 with a min-bill offer and a charge), non-money writes 1–3 → 0; a least-value bag tap stays at 5 (the SDK re-issues the freebie lock object — an equal-lock guard in `reconcileFreebieAllocation` would make it 3: SDK, money path, not asked) and a menu one-tap add at 3–5 (the added modal repeats the TOTAL; no cross-instance cache was built); the old key also kept a previous tab's bill until the next cart write (unreachable in the app — a tab change rides with a cart write; the golden test pins the stricter key). (2) overlay-shells (d66286f): REDUX-3 (a)–(c) — OfferRemovalNotice, ProductAddedModal and MakeItAMealPrompt render a one-selector shell while closed (engineering note): 148 useSelector calls per cart write on /menu → 0; CHECKOUT-2 — /tent commits on BACK or an accepted CONFIRM (NO THANKS commits ""), no per-key rewrite of the persisted root; REDUX-3 (d), the BagSheet shell (~392 selectors while the bag is closed), left for a decision (proposals row). The review found a PRE-EXISTING Rule 3 leak: the MIAM multi-combo pick survives the splash reset into the next guest's prompt (proposals row, P8). (3) paint-touch (aa10f87): PAINT-1 with opt-outs and PAINT-4 shipped (engineering note; the a11y row's S3 now names `touch-action` — a two-finger pinch had zoomed the kiosk to 2x); PAINT-3 / MENU-6 dropped (a 32 px box-shadow blurs at half drop-shadow's sigma: 58–62k px differ by up to 21 levels) and MENU-4 dropped (content-visibility moved the veg mark's edges by up to 60 levels) — the pixel proof caught what the audit had called 'visual none'; option A (the menu pane, rail and buy stage composited) left for a decision (P7). Reviews: all three shipped; the minors (the Tent and BagSheet comments) fixed at merge. Gate on 8cd1353 green; main fast-forwarded. (4) cold-yield (98f705b) + cold-yield2 (9e4cc26): the benchmark found ONE regression — the un-awaited Dexie write (BOOT-4) merged the menu response's parse, IndexedDB's structured clone of the menu (it runs as put() does) and the conversion into one task, so a COLD download's longest frame grew from ~230 to ~450 ms (the LoAF attribution: the conversion's IDBTransaction.oncomplete frame folded into Response.text.then); one setTimeout(0) after the write split off the conversion (~340 ms), a second before it split off the copy — now below the original (308 → 272 ms back-to-back); each test fails without its yield. Gates on 79cfd76 and d34bff4 green (the header) — the d34bff4 e2e ran 1.6 h because the Mac slept mid-gate (`net::ERR_NETWORK_IO_SUSPENDED`, 28 click / setup timeouts in specs the change cannot reach), and those 28 re-ran green with `--last-failed`; main fast-forwarded. Benchmark (`tests/e2e/perf.preview.ts`: production build, 6x CPU, --disable-gpu; the original code = branch perf-bench at 5f1db99 against the merged pass, run back-to-back on a quiet machine): the per-guest menu load on the 12 MB reference menu (the 304 path) TBT 69 → 14 ms, on screen 339 → 265 ms; the in-bag EAT IN ⇄ TAKE OUT switch (304) TBT 126 → 64 ms, on screen 309 → 240 ms; a long-menu rail tap to a far section 93 → 78 ms, its long frame gone (56 → 0 ms); opening the bag and the ADA view: no long frame (56–59 → 0 ms). The lanes' own measurements: a PDP fling 16 → 39 frames shown and the ADA bag list 40 → 2 main-thread commits per fling (paint-touch's traces, kiosk path), a bag "+" 5 → 3 bill runs (bill-memo), closed overlays 148 → 0 selector runs per cart write (overlay-shells). The cold download (first boot or a changed menu), after cold-yield2, back-to-back: longest frame 308 → 272 ms, on screen 1,183 → 1,087 ms (the order-type switch with a download 291 → 257 ms). Only n ≥ 2 row worse by > 20 ms: the warm reload's longest frame 179 → 217 ms (no boot code changed — noise or the +0.3 KiB entry). The results JSON live outside the repo (`~/.claude/projects/…/perf-pass/perf-{baseline2,final2,baseline3,final3}.json`).

- **2026-10-16 (the low-end perf pass, wave 1: the benchmark, the menu load, the menu grid — lanes perf-bench, data-hooks, menu-render merged)** · User 2026-10-16: "make sure every thing is highly optimized because it will run on also low end windows device, so all and every page should be optimized for it, run multiple agents to make sure all the things are optimized and nothing breaks in process". Wave 1, each lane build → adversarial review → fix: (1) perf-bench (5f1db99) — the low-end benchmark (engineering note); its reviewer found 16 measurement defects (frame thresholds on the vsync grid, lower-middle medians, single samples on hot spots, the 304 menu path never served, no long-menu scroll), all fixed; (2) data-hooks (209c58b) — BOOT-1 production freezes only the menu payload roots (Immer's freeze per pipeline tap 11–13 ms → 0.1 ms on the reference menu on the dev machine, several times that on the kiosk; the auditor's 41 ms came from nested drafts), BOOT-2 out of stock / server time / offers / DP sessions start before the Dexie read of the stored menu, BOOT-4 the cache write of a changed menu is not awaited, PDP-2 useCustomization calls the pure SDK functions and a shared `resolveModifierGroups` instead of instantiating useMenuConverters / useLoyalty / useAppSettings / a bare useMakeItAMeal (−64 % per re-render in a jsdom micro-benchmark), PDP-1 step 1 `memo(YouMightLikeRail)`; the offers-array freeze was left out (offer code); (3) menu-render (b446423) — MENU-1 (= REDUX-1, BAG-2) `memo(MenuItemCard)` + stable handlers: ~122 cards stop re-rendering on every cart write, rail tap and overlay toggle, the bag's included; BAG-8 a stable `onAdd` in CompleteYourMealRail; ENTRY-2 ActivityModal mounts at the visit's first open; ENTRY-1 (a memoized route tree) DROPPED by the orchestrator — its reviewer found the hook-less guards then missed the token flip, and the fix needed a cookie read in AppRoutes for no measurable gain (`AppRoutesRecovery.test.tsx` keeps the recovery pin). Gate on a6b9a09 green (the header: unit +12 tests, e2e unchanged, entry +0.1 KiB); main fast-forwarded. Wave 2 in lanes: bill-memo (M1) and paint-touch (scrollers composited on a 100 %-scaled panel, card photos' multiply isolated, content-visibility on the cards, the CTA bar's box-shadow, pinch-zoom and image drag blocked on Windows touch — pixel-identical by a screenshot diff); next: overlay-shells (REDUX-3 a–c, CHECKOUT-2), then baseline vs final on the benchmark, back-to-back.

- **2026-10-15 (the menu follows posistKiosk's layout settings: the sub-category rail, one section at a time — lane menu-subcat merged; the entry budget 365 KiB)** · User 2026-10-15: "in posistkiosk the view is subcategory on the basis of the settings which are on please integrate those". Lane menu-subcat (69ad689, 11db98f; fixer f31c527; merge c5d053b) — research, build, review, fix. Research read the original posistKiosk: its live menu page and the settings behind it (engineering note "The posistKiosk menu settings map"); the user's screenshot is its mode M4 (`send_categories` and `one_category_at_a_time` on, with the theme's big photo tiles). Built in `pages/Menu/index.tsx` and `components/menu/CategoryRail.tsx`, no SDK change (every selector, action and event already existed): (1) `send_categories` on (the menu's, per order type), unless `show_only_supercategories` — the rail lists the SUB-categories in posistKiosk's global order (the test menu interleaves its two categories: Boxes · Side Orders · Sauces · Kiddie Meal), each section headed by its own name (the H2, no H3 — 1:2595); the rows come from the sellable, filtered nested tree by id, so sold-out and dead items stay Unavailable, and an empty, missing or repeated sub-category gets no row; (2) `one_category_at_a_time` on — the pane shows the selected row's section alone; a tap swaps it, and any change of the shown section starts it at its top (an order-type switch or a refetch that drops the row too — f31c527); the selection lives in the SDK's in-view ids, so the PDP, the bag, Tent, Payment and Paytm returns keep it and the order-type pick clears it; the rail brings the selected row into view when its box hides it (on the return, and on the ADA toggle — f31c527); (3) settings off = today's view, byte for byte (rows, ids, sections, anchor scroll, `SupCategoryClicked`); a sub-category tap sends `SubCategoryClicked` (the id, the untranslated name). The shared e2e menu now serves `send_categories: false` (69ad689), so every existing spec stays on the category view with no assertion changed; the converter's golden test keeps the tenant's true (pins unchanged). Review (1 major, 5 minor), all answered: F1 the builder's sign-off and question 1 said posistKiosk's photos come only with the theme flag — its sub-category rows ALWAYS carry one (a 48 px thumbnail by default, the 112×128 tile with `show_big_category_image`), reworded before they reached the user; F2 the ADA toggle hid a low selected row (the rail re-reveals on a resize); F3 one at a time, a section change with no tap kept the old pane scroll, so the guest landed mid-grid with no heading (the reset now follows the shown section); F4 `send_categories` off with one at a time on is a third layout, and `send_categories` varies by order type — answered by decision (b); F5 five new `any`s → a local `MenuNode` type; F6 the e2e scroll-reset check passed without the reset (the one-at-a-time menus lengthened, the overflow asserted first). User decisions 2026-10-15 on the lane's questions, all keeping what was built: (a) the rail keeps Figma's text buttons, no photos — a deliberate divergence from posistKiosk; (b) `send_categories` off with one at a time on shows one whole category per tap (posistKiosk: a +/− accordion, one sub-category at a time); (c) the full-width banner, the filter row and the order-type name above the rail stay out (the sub-category row; for sign-off: the Round 3 sign-off list (15)–(21)). Tests: Menu.test 15 new (a 7-row settings → rail / pane table and the behaviour: the H2, the tap, the event, the selection across a remount and at the order-type pick, the reset on a store change, the sellable tree, Arabic aliases, one scroll, categories one at a time; `resetStore()` resets to the settings-off boot), CategoryRail.test 2 (the reveal at mount and on a resize), new `tests/e2e/menuSubcategories.spec.ts` (17: 5 setting combinations × EN / AR / ADA, the tenant's view through a sold-out card, a one-tap add, the meal prompt into the PDP and back and the bag, and the ADA long rail); without the change 13 of the build's 15 new unit tests and 14 of the 17 e2e fail (the rest assert the settings-off view, which holds both ways), and with both pane resets removed all 9 one-at-a-time e2e runs fail. Boot path +0.5 KiB: the CI entry 360.3 KiB, over the 360 — user decision 2026-10-15: raise the entry budget to 365 KiB (f7bcf56; `checkBundleSize.test` pins 365) over the lodash trim again; the trim stays open (the bundle-headroom row). An offer whose buy side can never be completed now shows exactly like a sold-out one — the gone row: struck, "Not available right now", no description, no ADD ITEMS, never staged, never auto-applied, listed below the eligible and locked rows. Relation rule, as for sold out: one dead entry kills a plain "and" side, an "or" side needs every entry dead; a mixed "or" side keeps its locked row and its stage adds only the live tiles (the open calls row (1)). Review (1 major, 1 minor), fixed: least-value and group-wise buy sides are unions in the engine (`getMatchingBuyQuantity`), auto-remove and the stage, but `getBuySideState` read `rawItems[0].relation`, so an "and" offer over a live burger and a dead box was struck though the bag qualified — SAVE could not pick it, auto-apply refused it — and 7ef8270 judges both mechanics as unions (dead only when every entry is), the converter's stamp included (for sign-off: the open calls row (2)); the builder's note that an item 86'd mid-session strikes at once held only for an OPTION PAY's re-check finds out (the offers-parity row (7), corrected). The GET side is unchanged: a get side that can never be granted stays hidden (`hasUnresolvableGetSide`, from the converter's stamps — known limits (d)). With a least-value offer ("buy N, your cheapest free", or V % off) applied, each paid bag row it covers shows posistKiosk's ribbon wording (CartItem / QuickAccessItem) on the bag's purple chip — the freebie / reward rows' pill, now one `CHIP` constant: the whole row "Free", some units "{n} Free", below 100 % "{v}% off", or "{v}% off ×{n}" for some units (`offers.unitsFree`, `offers.percentOffUnits`; AR drafts) — one chip per row, the quantity never split, and the row's line price struck above the live one (one price column for every row; testids `bag-least-chip-*`, `bag-was-*`, `bag-price-*`, none starting "bag-row-"). Root cause: TB loaded `get_out_of_stock` exactly as posistKiosk does (with getMenu at /second and on the /menu re-drive, again at PAY with the bag's ids; the converter stamps items, sizes and every constituent item), but only MenuItemCard read the stamp: the PDP grid and rows, its size grid, Select a Size, the slot sheet and the tier-2 page offered out-of-stock options and pre-picked out-of-stock defaults, and PAY removed an out-of-stock pick without naming it, so the next PAY went through without it. (1) Options: one SDK predicate, `isOptionOutOfStock` (`@cx-sdk/catalog/oos/optionStock`; PAY's feed map wins over the stamp for addon / combo / vaddon — posistKiosk's overlay), on every chooser: an out-of-stock option card or stepper row is greyed with the "Unavailable" chip, no radio / CHOOSE / CUSTOMIZE, sorted last (`stockLast`), named "<name>, unavailable"; sizes alike on the PDP grid and in Select a Size (focus on the X when every size is out; deltas from the cheapest size in stock); the slot sheet, and an empty slot with nothing in stock; never pre-picked, never opened by `preselectedVariantId` or as a lone size; Edit deselects what went out, nested picks too; (2) the bag: PAY keeps a pick its sweep dropped in its row, flagged (SDK `sweptPicks.keepSweptPicksFlagged`, total_price recomputed the engine's way), and names it — the red "Out of stock: <names>" line, the pick's own line red, Edit (a reward row: Remove); the PAY notice names the item, its size or the picks; PAY is disabled and aria-busy while it checks; (3) w4 #1: the server clock — the one network step that could throw into PAY's catch — falls back to the device clock (reported), and every step reports ErrorOccurred `{error_source: "checkout", stage}`, DEV builds keeping the last 50 events on `window.__kioskEvents` (0d745b5; no console — Rule 4 / C5); the cause stays a theory (not reproduced); (4) decision 6b (1e07a67, 4d1a788): when PAY's check cannot run (a failed chunk or feed, a non-list, any throw), the flags the live rows carry block with the named notice — fail-open (D-A1-1) only past what no check has flagged; (5) decision 6a (b580741, a3801ce): the order-type switch keeps a row whose add-on the new tab sells out of, flagged and named (SDK `orderTypeSwitch.ts` opt-in `keepSoldOutPicks`), every row the tab serves sheds the old tab's flags (`freshStockFlags`), and the burst clears PAY's feed map and both lists; (6) decision 6c (fecc299, b7b7760, 52f2a47, 611fe86, cccd9d6, 3208e8f): an item no guest can complete — a required group with nothing in stock — reads out of stock through one SDK rule (`hasDeadRequiredGroup` / `markUncompletable`; engineering note "The dead rule") at every entity source: the menu card, quick add, Select a Size, the rails, the added-modal strip, buy-stage tiles (one size, or every size a tile lists), the meal prompt (the add decision counts only the combos the prompt can offer, so it never opens without a meal) and the freebie picker (a dead freebie dropped, as a sold-out one is); a sized item reads Unavailable only when no size is left to complete; (7) 2153df6 put Select a Size back on the boot path (ddc672a's lazy move had kept the lane under 355; with the 360 budget, c0a95af, the move only added a bare "Close" scrim when the chunk was late or failed). Reviews — round 1 (F1–F10), round 2 (2 blockers, 2 majors, 1 minor) and oos-2 (5 minor) — all fixed: a stock check that could not run checked a flagged pick out (1e07a67); an Edit of a sized row kept its red line until the next PAY (99b7cfc — `releasePayVerdicts` in both commit branches); the tab switch's stale flags (above); test holes pinned (tier-2 stepper rows, the slot sheet's Customize, the sized Edit, the slot sheet's feed map); PAY's entry guard moved inside the try and its unreachable label removed (5527b6f); oos-2's meal prompt with no meal, all-dead sized card, narrowed category tile and dead freebie. Merge (8f9d86f): 5 textual conflicts, both sides kept — the no-photo bell dims to 40 % on an unavailable option, row, slot row or size tile; a Select a Size test re-expressed in posistKiosk's whole-amount format ("+£1"). Tests: the new CustomizationStock, optionStock and useAddEntityToCartCombos files; cases in BagSheetStock, BagItemRow, checkoutStockSweep, menuSellableEntity, ProductAddedModalDeadGroups, SelectSizeModal, Tier2CustomizationSheet, OptionCard, BuyStageSheet, FreebiePickerSheet, RepeatItemSheet, Menu and orderTypeSwitch; the new tests/e2e/outOfStock.spec (MENU + PDP, the dead pack, MIAM, BAG, PAY busy, a failed server clock) and orderTypeSwitch.spec OUT OF STOCK ON THE NEW TAB; five specs now break PAY's preflight with `pages/order` `breakPayPreflight`, since a failed clock no longer fails PAY; hand mutants killed at every step (47 / 47 on the first build). SDK: `optionStock.ts`, `orderTypeSwitch.ts` (uncommitted; `sweptPicks.ts` is in the snapshot). Sign-off: the "Round 3 sign-off list" row (1)–(8), (14); open: the two out-of-stock rows (open calls, known limits). Gate: the bag-ribbon entry's (f5a421c).

- **2026-10-14 (the payment-start failure modal closes in place — lane pay-close merged)** · User 2026-10-14: "there is no option to get back and try another payment when this comes please do that" / "instead of back to cart it should be just close". Lane pay-close (c734115; fixer 3ce71cc; merge 2bdf3bb): /payment's UNABLE TO PROCESS PAYMENT modal (the Paytm initiate failure, on the 1:6288 layout) offers CLOSE · TRY AGAIN for a retryable failure — busy, or an initiate the server rejected ("busy" is retryable: the SDK's `decidePaytmInitiate` marks every server failure so; only local refusals are not) — and CLOSE alone for a local refusal (no usable row, a zero amount, the screen chunk missing, an order-details mismatch, a local error); focus on CLOSE, because TRY AGAIN mints new ids and may arm the card machine; CLOSE (`language.close`) is the old PAY ANOTHER WAY handler renamed `handlePaytmClose` — the failure back to idle, the 500 ms tap guard re-armed, the payment type cleared (the attempt's ids were already cleared and flushed) — so the guest is back on the tiles, free to pick PAY AT COUNTER or the same tile; `handlePaytmBackToBag` and `paytm.failed.payAnotherWay` are deleted. Fixer (review F1): the CLOSE-alone refusal no longer asks for a retry — `paytm.failed.startNoRetry` "We couldn't start the payment. Please pay another way." / "تعذّر بدء الدفع. يرجى اختيار طريقة دفع أخرى." (AR draft). Left as they are: /paymentPolling's not-paid panel (its TRY AGAIN already returns to the tiles; its BACK TO MY BAG stays — review F2, a product call on the sign-off list), the unknown and staff panels (money may have moved), the pay-at-counter order-error panels (an order-placement failure). posistKiosk: its initiate error closes in place with the modal's X. Rule 1 transitions: the modal's /payment → /cart edge is removed; the bag is reached through /payment's BACK (since back-nav the previous step: the bag directly with CRM hidden and no table, else step by step). The user's decision overrides SDK CLAUDE.md Rule 2's literal "retry or return to cart" for the CLOSE-alone variant: CLOSE returns to the tiles, where PAY AT COUNTER and BACK remain. - **2026-10-14 (long category names grow their rail row; headings break long words; no native rail scrollbar — lane rail-wrap merged)** · User 2026-10-14: "some category name which are big eg. New Herfy Super Category , is getting cut please fix" and "there is a scrollbar in the sidebar and in the bottom the category item is getting cut". Lane rail-wrap (f79e8bc; fixer 9f747c6; 725eeb5; merge b892c96): every rail row was a fixed `h-[64px]` with Cm Bd 30/24 in the 179 px text box, so the tenant's "NEW HERFY SUPER CATGEORY" (3 lines, 72 px) overflowed — a `<button>` centres its content only when it fits, so the third line sat at 48–72 of 64, clipped by the list on the last row and over the next row elsewhere — and one long word ran 301 px wide. Now `min-h-[64px] py-[8px]` + `[overflow-wrap:anywhere]` (the D14 `pl-[24px] pr-[12px]` kept): 1- and 2-line names keep their 64 px pill, lines in the same place, 3 lines make it 88 px and each further line adds 24, the selected pill (the row's own fill) grows with it, and a word wider than the box breaks inside it ("NEWHERFYSUPE / RCATEGORY"). The review found the same symptom in the section headings — an over-long one-word category name ran the 64 px h2 off screen and made the menu pane scroll sideways (1209 > 816) — so the section h2 and the sub-category h3 break an over-long word too (9f747c6); and the rail list hides its native scrollbar like the menu pane, touch scrolling kept (725eeb5 — the user's second report: the cut row's overflow had made the list scrollable). Tests: CategoryRail unit (no h- / max-h- class, the new classes, the pill, no scrollbar), Menu unit (the headings' class), the new tests/e2e/railLongNames.spec (EN / AR / ADA: every line box inside its row, nothing past the box, row height = max(64, 16 + 24 × lines), labels centred within 1 px, headings and the pane never wider than their box, no scrollbar painted) — each fails with its fix reverted. (4) Checkout — two PAY AT COUNTER orders on the test tenant: #62850 (EN guest: Noodles QUARTER + Latte + Cold Coffee, Happy Hour 25 % auto-applied, ﷼15.36) and #88533 (Arabic + ADA: Latte + Cold Coffee + SM Americano, Happy Hour, ﷼11.82); CRM is skipped on the tenant (straight to /payment; 2 methods, only PAY AT COUNTER tapped); /receipt NO. The idle tab reloaded itself once on /second (≈13:50 IST, cause unknown — open row). 3 findings (1 major, 2 minor). In all 44 findings (20 major, 24 minor), overlapping across parts (the rial bidi, the CTA count and TOTAL, the " ?" typo, the least-value copy, the size-tile price, BACK TO MENU). - **2026-10-14 (the buy stage's cards are Figma's Menu-Item card; Order Complete counts START NEW ORDER down, without the photo — lanes stage-cards + success-timer merged)** · Two user requests, one lane each (contract → build → adversarial review → fix). (1) stage-cards — user: "the item card in get stage in offer doesn't look good make it something like ui in figma". On the tenant every buy group is one item named after itself, so each card sat alone under a header repeating its name, two thirds of the row empty. BuyStageSheet's card is now Figma's Menu-Item grid card (1:2609, MenuItemCard's encoding: 240×277 grey-6 r8, the Md 20/24 name clamped at 2, `formatMenuPriceLine` led by `VegMark`, the 172 photo band object-cover × multiply, Figma's 36 px Quick Add at 192/12 on a 44 px hit area), four across in 1:2601's 16 px gaps, centred; picked the product-added way — the whole card adds (an empty overlay named "Quick add" / "Customize" <name> from the SDK's side-effect-free `isDirectlyAddable`; the "+" its aria-hidden twin), then the purple ring, ForYouCard's pill at bottom-start (an "N selected" status) and the "−" at bottom-end (trash at 1, minus from 2); unavailable = the menu card's look, no add target; logical insets; `offers.buyStage.add` / `.increase` removed (EN + AR); handlers, guards and SDK calls unchanged — no SDK edit, no new dependency. Review (1 blocker, 1 major, 4 minor), all fixed in 6cd23eb: the header rule's displayed-name compare was untested (a variant entry's "<name> (<size>)" case added — deleting the compare now fails it); a lone self-named item after a headed category sat under that category's heading and read as one more of its options (the per-group rule + a `col-start-1` row break) → headers are decided for the WHOLE stage (any group that needs one — a category, a label the card does not show, a per-group need above 1 in plain-and / plain-or — heads every group; else none, the tenant shape), the row-break code deleted; the Unavailable chip overlapped a picked card's pill and "−" by 6 / 5 px → it rises to bottom-56 when qty > 0; rows of 1–3 cards hugged the left edge → `repeat(auto-fit,240px)` centres them (E4 asserts the pair's midpoint is the dialog's centre — 284 vs 540 on the old grid); no capture rendered a header → capture 37 `offers-<mode>-37-buy-stage-headed` (EN / AR / ADA: 2 headers, each spanning the 1008 px grid); the stale E1 "ADD" wording. Tests: unit BuyStageSheet 18 → 39 (13/13 builder mutants and the fixer's M1–M9 killed); e2e offerBuyStage E3 / E4 / E9 extended; visual-offers' least offer in the tenant shape (capture 27, no headers) + the headed capture 37. (2) success-timer — user: "at the order success screen show timer and remove the static image in the middle from there". Removed: the 412×900 photo panel (normal + ADA), the guest's UNLOCK FREEBIES WITH POINTS callout + arrow (it pointed into the photo), their 3 assets (out of the precache) and the 3 `success.unlock*` keys (EN + AR). One rule centres the text block on the page (the stage, or ADA's reach zone) instead of Figma's top 345. START NEW ORDER counts down — "START NEW ORDER · 20S" (AR "… · 20 ث", Western digits in one `dir="auto"` leaf): ONE clock, a deadline fixed at mount read by a 1 s interval that also fires the exit at 0 (the separate 20 s setTimeout is gone), stopped once the exit is decided and on unmount; a tap still exits at once, a touch never extends it; the name stays "Start new order" (the digits aria-hidden). Behind the label `.tb-countdown-fill` slides in from the left over `--tb-countdown` (fed from `ORDER_SUCCESS_COUNTDOWN_SECONDS`): one linear CSS transform run that nothing waits on, hidden under reduced motion (the digits carry it). `success.countdown` / `success.countdownA11y` new (EN + AR). Review (2 major, 3 minor), fixed in 68490f5: F1 the sr-only status mounted already filled, which screen readers skip (the repo's own rule: a live region exists before its text) → it mounts empty and the first tick fills it once with that tick's real seconds (latched: a late tick never announces a stale 19); F2 the white 24 px label under the 20 % fill fell to 2.93:1 on tb-orchid (large text needs 3:1) → 15 % (3.12:1 on the token, 3.16–3.20:1 measured over the sheen), guarded by a new contrast.test pair that reads the alpha from the page; F3 `[dir="rtl"] .tb-countdown-fill` could never match (nothing sets dir=rtl — "RTL text, LTR layout") → deleted: the fill grows from the left in EN and AR, like the idle prompt's START AGAIN, and motionCss pins that nothing re-aims it; F4 the AppRoutes /orderSuccess comment ("no controls") rewritten; F5 the stale doc spots (the QR and Motion rows, BACKEND_CONTRACT_PROPOSALS §2) — applied in this docs pass. Tests: unit OrderSuccess (the count, the deadline catch-up, the tap, the constant name, the status empty at mount then filled once, the fill, Arabic, no panel / unlock), motionCss and contrast.test pins; e2e orderComplete.spec rewritten (the label ticks 20 → 15 at 5 s → 1 at 19 s → /start at 20 s on the page clock; a tap at 17 exits at once; the status empty on arrival, the 19 s sentence after 1 s; the fill one 20,000 ms linear transform run at −1030 / −515 / 0 px at 0 / 10 / 20 s, 15 % tb-surface, none under reduced motion; the exit at 0 with the fill paused; AR Western digits; ADA at the zone's foot; capture oc-06 half-filled at 10 s), motion M0 (1 entering block) / M11 (the fill never restarts on the ADA exit), `settle()` exempting the running fill. Lane gates: validate green (stage-cards 217 files / 3,724; success-timer 3,716); e2e offerBuyStage 19/19 + offers / loyaltyOfferLock / visual-offers 46/46 (stage-cards); orderComplete 6/6, motion 24/24 and every other spec that reaches Order Complete 186/186 (success-timer — its full run stopped at 463/545 when the machine slept; the rest ran separately, 4 visual-offers ADA load timeouts passing 8/8 alone). - **2026-10-13 (offers parity — lane offers-parity merged)** · User 2026-10-13: "why buy stage is not opening in some of this offers ? please check posistKiosk for refrences m also full descriptions are also not showing please add full descriptions , these are all the offers which are comig from backend" (76 offers). Built tests-first (13 e2e + U1–U12 red on 1d3dea8) in five commits: (1) F1 — get-category groups in FreebiePickerSheet (a section per group, exact grants via `buildCategoryCommitLines`, a customizable entity one unit per group (D7), "or" offers switch options without wiping the active group) and ADD ITEMS → buy stage for get-category BOGOs (`canOfferBuyStageWithCategories`, only when the picker can ever complete the get side — `canCompleteGetSide`), with the crash-reload sweep keeping the applied offer's category freebies (`findOrphanedFreebieRowsWithCategories`) in the same commit; (2) F2 + D3 — ADD ITEMS on a min-items lock while the buy side is unmet, the stage names any other gap at once and CONTINUE waits for it; item minimums count paid units (freebie rows excluded, as revalidation check 3 does); (3) F3 — descriptions: SDK `buildBuyGetDescription` (clauses as data) + TB `useOfferDescription` (lazy, 20 `offers.*` families EN + AR; own description → buy/get sentence → item-criteria scope line; locked scoped rows "Not valid on …" / "Add something from …"; Arabic composed runs are RTL isolates so they read in order); OfferRow clamps at 2 lines with a measured Show more / Show less (Figma 1:4034 / 1:4069; token `tb-grey-1`; none on a D3-locked row) and the eligible row became an overlay radio (D8: `offer-row-<id>` is the radio, described by the Applied pill + the second line + the description; the row's text is `offer-row-body-<id>`); (4) F4 — `rankOffersForKiosk` hides offers that can never unlock on this menu (a dead buy side the menu doesn't carry, a configured get side that resolves to nothing, or a category group the picker can never fill — D7) and turns a sold-out buy side into the struck gone row, judged at rank time from the menu maps (no converter stamp); the bag's Rewards row never names a gone offer; (5) F5 — a category offer's "Save up to" is what the picker can give (the same-or-less ceiling and D7, priced on a copy — the ranked entry keeps the original offer, so the stage and SAVE re-apply the ceiling on the live cart); the item-criteria caps in the scope line. - **2026-10-13 (/forYou dropped — lane drop-foryou merged)** · User request 2026-10-11: "drop /foryou screen" (part 1 of 4; motion and Order Complete are their own lanes). Lane `drop-foryou`, on the reviewed final contract: VIEW MY BAG always opens the bag (`handleViewBag`: `if (totalQuantity > 0) navigate("/cart")`; an empty bag stays a no-op), so Complete Your Meal lives only in the bag, as Figma 1:3171 draws it; `/forYou` is now `<Navigate to="/cart" replace />`, never NotFound (Rule 1: chunkRecovery's preload-error reload of the CURRENT URL could land a guest with a full bag on it) — with items the guest lands on the bag, with an empty bag the bag closes itself to /menu once the Dexie rehydrate settles. Rule 1 transitions: menu → /forYou removed; menu → /cart unconditional; a stale /forYou → /cart added; the splash, the 120 s idle reset and the error screen unchanged. Deleted: `pages/ForYou` (page + unit test), `tests/e2e/forYou.spec.ts` (6 tests), the `upsell` and `customization` copy blocks (EN + AR; `customization.comingSoon` had no reader), the session reset's `resetCartUpsellSeen` and the store's do-not-persist note (nothing here writes `cartUpsellSeen` / `cartUpsellEntryQuantity` any more; the SDK state stays for the fork); commit 2 swept the telemetry-only path (`getBreakdown`, the breakdown / exclusions wrappers, the grid-capacity math and its tests). Analytics — tell the analytics owners: this repo no longer emits `cart_upsell_viewed`, `cart_upsell_suppressed` or `cart_upsell_skipped` (the SDK enum stays for the fork); viewed / suppressed were the only per-tap "VIEW MY BAG" signal — `cart_viewed` and /cart's `pageViewed` still fire, but they also count returns to the bag from later screens; a session that used to pass /forYou sends one `MenuViewed` fewer (/menu and /cart share one Menu instance, so closing the bag no longer re-mounts the menu); a stale /forYou URL still logs one `pageViewed` with route_path "/forYou" before the redirect. `enable_cart_upsell_screen: false` now hides two rails, together: the bag's Complete-Your-Meal rail (both sources, the isCartRecommended items and the tenant list) and the PDP's You Might Like rail; the product-added strip and the rewards sheet's Suggested rail ignore it (pre-existing); proposal §4 withdrawn. ForYouCard stays (the bag and PDP rails). Review fixes (two adversarial reviews): the bag's CTA tap guard (`bag-cta-tap-guard`, 500 ms) now also arms when the bag OPENS — the menu's VIEW MY BAG overlaps PAY by a 4–5 px band (330 px wide; 214 px with loyalty), so a double tap there started checkout (/phone), which the drop made reachable on every session's first bag open (pre-existing on later opens); a bag that MOUNTS open (/cart reached from another route, e.g. the PDP's UPDATE, which overlaps PAY by 3 px) is still unguarded — pre-existing, open; a deliberate PAY / LOG-IN tap inside that 0.5 s is swallowed too — sign-off; the Arabic walk's order-24 bag capture waits for the rail (it captured none; the wrapped calorie line was only clip-checked later, under the rewards sheet); bag.spec checks that the redirect REPLACES; `resolveCustomizationReturnPath`'s `returnPath` (no screen sets it now; kept for the SDK add intent) gets a unit pin. Tests: a Menu unit guard (VIEW MY BAG → /cart and never /forYou with a cart-recommended item, and no cart_upsell_* event; an empty bag stays put) and bag.spec "NO /forYou" (a script pop on an empty and on a full bag — the history entry replaced — then a full load of /forYou) — both fail on the base; bag.spec "DOUBLE TAP" and a BagSheetOffers unit test fail with the bag-open guard reverted; the twelve openBag copies narrowed to /cart; the pill-covers-no-text check moved from visual-offers' /forYou walk to the PDP rail (bag-recommendations); the /forYou captures (order-12, order-23, offers-*-12) dropped, not renumbered. Gate on the lane: lint, type-check, guardrails (0 critical / 117 warnings), depcruise, check-deps and unit (211 files / 3,463) green, e2e 504/504 in 13.3 min (`--workers=2`), both builds, boot path 350.4 KiB (CI 352.0, −1.1) / 355, page JS 1,349.8 KB, the SDK checksum listing (737 files) unchanged, fork `tsc -b` green. Mutation check (sandbox on 5403): 17 mutants of the contract's decisions and the review fixes, all killed; the one survivor (an upsell event re-emitted on VIEW MY BAG) got an analytics assertion in the Menu guard. Merged together with lane order-success (gate in the entry above).

- **2026-10-12 (product-added CTA names its tap; one hop on the PDP; the size hand-off)** · User report (a screenshot of a customized add: no strip, "Total ﷼131.50", ADD TO ORDER): "why add ot order button is coming when there is no item to add to order". Two ultracode workflows. (1) Design: 3 read-only scouts — Figma (no variant of 1:4589: one filled CTA, X the only other exit), posistKiosk (no strip: an outlined "Okay" with a 6 s countdown; a strip with nothing picked: "No, Thanks" + a disabled "Add to Order"), TB (states, tests, existing keys) — a designer, and 3 critics: the parity critic replaced the proposed CONTINUE ORDERING with posistKiosk's words, and the UX critic found a BLOCKER in the staging itself — the PDP's own "You Might Like" rail stayed on a suggestion's PDP, so a rail tap parked a row the modal never showed (ADD TO ORDER with nothing visibly picked) or bumped an in-bag row straight into the bag (e2e runs with the tenant map off). (2) A leak sweep for that class: 3 finders (call sites, on-screen surfaces, state lifecycle), 2 skeptics per finding — 3 confirmed (one root cause: Select a Size's CONTINUE into the PDP closed the sheet first while RR7's transition still showed the menu, so a double tap's second half added the card beneath — or, in ADA, opened the bag with the detour set, where adds parked unseen), 2 refuted (the MIAM prompt cannot open from a suggestion). Fixes: the CTA reads ADD TO ORDER only while something is picked (`hasPicks`), else NO THANKS over a strip (`added.noThanks`, new) / GOT IT without one (`offers.gotIt`); the PDP hides its rail during a suggestion's detour; Select a Size stays up as the cover through its hand-off to the PDP and a second CONTINUE does nothing. Tests: unit — the CTA in every state (EN + AR), the PDP rail during a detour, the hand-off cover (Menu, the fast-lane option test); e2e — CTA labels in P1 / P2 / P6, P3b ONE HOP (tenant recs through the DEV seam), P7c HAND-OFF (a size with options, a dblclick over the first card column); mutation — P3b fails without the rail gate (the rail renders), P7c fails with the sheet closed first (Tortilla Sauce lands in the bag, the detour cleared). Load-flaky, seen at load 12–17 while the orchestrator's lanes ran, then green: CustomizationPack "SAVE on a pick with a REQUIRED nested group" (3 fails, then 4/4) and BagSheetOffersLane (once). Gate (main's tree, TB_E2E_PORT 5379): validate green (unit 211 files / 3,544; guardrails 0 critical / 118 warnings), e2e 511/511 in 7.1 min, both builds, boot path 352.3 KiB (CI 353.9) / 355, page JS 1,356.0 KB; no SDK edit, no new dependency. Sign-off: items (5), (13)–(15) of the staged-selection row.

No SDK edit, no new dependency. - **2026-10-01 (P9b)** · **Rule 2 hardening — every request is now bounded.**
  9-agent workflow (build ×3 → integrate → adversarial review ×2 → fix →
  unit + e2e authors; gates run directly). **Money bug fixed first (P7c
  regression):** a transport failure on `redeem_coupon` was read as SUCCESS
  — `finalLoyaltyRedemption` wrapped `infoForClaim` around an empty body, so
  the customer got the free reward row, the claim, and the points deduction
  without Xeno confirming; the sheet now also requires `status_code === 200`
  (fork parity). **SDK:** `configureKioskTransport()` gained opt-in
  `requestTimeoutMs` / `endpointTimeoutsMs` (TB: 10 s, `getMenu` 30 s; the
  fork configures nothing and was A/B-proven byte-identical on both RTK
  versions), `isTimeoutError` (TIMEOUT_ERROR, RTK 2.12's mid-body
  PARSING_ERROR, `.cause` chains), `isOrderPushOutcomeUnknown` +
  `shouldRetryOrderPush` (a timed-out push, or a 2xx whose body was lost, is
  NEVER auto-retried — uncertain-copy order-error panel, Retry reuses the same
  order id; clean 5xx keep the 4-attempt ladder), auth copy for timeouts.
  **Recovery surfaces** on one Figma-backed `ErrorModal` (1:3427 → Icon Modal
  1:988 + warning icon 1:990): menu-load failure keeps the guest on `/second`
  with TRY AGAIN (was a permanent "Menu is loading…"); `/menu` refetches on a
  reload with no menu; `/LoadingResources` shows categorised translated errors
  and auto-retries 10 → 30 → 60 s then every 60 s, on reconnect, or TRY AGAIN
  NOW ("Back to registration" removed — it landed on an un-booted splash);
  `ErrorBoundary` is translated, recovers to `/start` after 10 s (to a fresh
  boot after 60 s when the crash is within a minute of load — no storage) and
  START OVER works offline. **Loyalty boot degrades** (user decision): a dead
  partner no longer blocks boot; the splash retries it once per visit; the
  Activity Center shows "Unavailable". Xeno revoke bounded at 10 s, never
  retried, one in flight per claim, ids in its analytics. **Found by the e2e
  author and fixed:** the new modal's `autoFocus` restarted the idle timer
  (IdleGuard now ignores `focus` events). Node pin bumped to 22 LTS (user
  decision) — `yarn validate` is green end-to-end for the first time.
  Unit 504→703 (+199, 34 deliberate breakages each caught), e2e 65→73
  (`recovery.spec.ts`: menu 500 / menu hang / boot retry / loyalty down /
  push timeout = exactly one request / push 5xx ladder / redeem timeout grants
  nothing / offline).

  9-agent workflow (build ×2 → integrate → adversarial review ×2 → fix →

  route around every in-session screen — one react-idle-timer, the library is
  the only clock), `components/common/IdleTimeoutModal` (Figma 1:4514; the
  START AGAIN "Loading" fill IS the countdown), `hooks/utils/useIdleTimeout`
  (`resolveIdleSeconds` — Rule 1 as a ceiling, `useIdleHold` with a 120 s
  cap). **Found by the trace and fixed:** TB's `/start` never reset the
  session (the fork's does), so the `/second` and `/phone` footer cancels
  leaked language, ADA, loyalty identity and — from `/phone` at checkout — the
  whole cart into the next session; the language never returned to English
  after a reset; the global error flag survived resets; boot threw on a
  missing `ideal_time`. **Found by the reviewers and fixed:** late loyalty
  lookups, menu fetches and PAY preflights writing into the NEXT customer's
  session after an idle exit, and the hold cap leaving `/orderSuccess`
  exposed to a stale idle period. Fork idle defects not ported: prompt at
  half time (reset at 65 s for `ideal_time` 120), three clocks, a 10 s
  warning (WCAG needs 20 s), teardown from an untracked `setTimeout`.
  Unit 422→504 (+82 incl. 20 deliberate breakages each caught), e2e 53→65
  (idle timeout, CONTINUE, H1 regression from `/cart`, START AGAIN, splash
  never prompts, push hold + hold cap, Arabic→English, exactly-one revoke,
  three late-result guards). Correction: guardrails reports 197 warnings
  (0 critical), not the "single warning" the P7c entry recorded.

  imports — every such string in `src/` is a comment recording what is

  (units, not rows) and emits CartUpsellViewed/Suppressed/Skipped. (3) **Overlay
  hoist** — MIAM + repeat sheet moved out of the Menu page to an
  `InSessionOverlays` layout route, fixing dead taps on /forYou (see

  proposed e2e fix that would have disabled the in-bag rail along with the

- **2026-09-30 (fix: kiosk images)** · User reported placeholder glyphs instead
  of item images on a real (Taco Bell) deployment. Root cause: every TB surface
  bound `entity.image_url`, which the backend sets to a generic S3 placeholder
  for most items, while the kiosk's real image lives in
  `aggregator_image[kiosk]` (what the fork renders). Added
  `src/utils/entityImage.ts` and swapped **21 render sites across 13
  components** (menu hero/grid cards, PDP hero + option + stepper + variant
  tiles, size modal, added modal, pack slot + slot sheet, tier-2 sheet, MIAM
  prompt, bag rows, Complete-Your-Meal rail, offers suggested rail, freebie
  picker, loyalty reward tiles). Items with no kiosk image now fall to their
  existing grey spacer instead of a broken-looking glyph. Unit 218→239 (16
  resolver contract tests + 5 render pins incl. the exact reported case);
  e2e 37/37 unchanged; build green; gates passed first attempt.
